- Status: Superseded in production by `D-2026-07-19-08`; one-Agent/current-context evidence ownership remains active, while `finish_research` and terminal synthesis do not.
- Refined by: `D-2026-07-19-02` gives DirectFinal and explicit finish one exact last-mile contract; `D-2026-07-19-05` replaces the empty finish object with a tolerant current-turn provenance handoff. Final review in `docs/adr/0004-agent-owned-research-loop.md` makes entity/quote/asset-route coverage advisory and prohibits using either route state or handoff resolution to veto a complete natural answer. Agent-loop ownership and the prohibition on post-run Interactive semantic review remain unchanged.
- Supersedes: `docs/adr/0003-isolated-agent-research-control.md` and every isolated `continue_research` / `finish_research` completion or control-error-to-terminal transition. It also replaces the sole/mixed routing details in `D-2026-07-18-03`; that decision's same-context evidence ownership, speculative tool-round boundary, tool-free terminal, committed-prefix transport recovery, exact visible/persisted body, and observational-only Interactive publication rule remain active.
- Incident evidence: After the MiniMax clean-EOF adapter repair was fully gated and deployed, a fresh production replay of `crwv和英伟达有什么关系` completed as one clean stream without a reset, second answer, fixed refusal, or missing-DONE error. Its business trace contained only `data_fetch(search)` for CRWV and NVIDIA. The separate control phase then produced an error-labelled implicit finish, and terminal synthesis asserted relationship, concentration, and infrastructure facts that no current-turn relationship source supported. This was a reasoning-protocol failure, not stale deployment, DataFetch/FMP outage, or transport failure.
- Agent-loop ownership: Active finance research uses one `Agent::run`, one context, and one model role. Business tools remain available in every active completion. Once structurally eligible, the internal `finish_research` schema appears alongside those tools; there is no separate `continue_research` model request and no service-authored implicit finish.
- Structural evidence ledger: `data_fetch(search)` is `identity_only`; the ledger separately records post-search quote/profile, crypto, broad-market, Web, and other attempts. This state is prompt guidance and telemetry, not finish or publication authority. Genuine no-coverage is recorded so the Agent can disclose it, while malformed and budget-rejected calls do not pretend that a provider was attempted.
- Sole explicit finish: Exactly one sole `finish_research` call whose arguments parse as an empty JSON object is the only path into empty-tools terminal synthesis. Mixed finish plus business calls executes the business calls; duplicate, malformed, nonempty-argument, or unavailable finish signals are ignored and never enter the actor registry, tool budget, observer, progress stream, or persistence.
- Agent-owned DirectFinal: Any complete nonempty natural-language body ending with provider `Stop + Done` in an active business completion is the same Agent's DirectFinal even when the advisory ledger is incomplete. It publishes once through the deferred Interactive path and triggers no retry, semantic veto, replacement, empty-tools terminal synthesis, or second model generation. Empty output, provider error, timeout, lifecycle error, or iteration exhaustion remain genuine failed runs and can never synthesize finish authority or DirectFinal success.
- Evidence and language boundary: The complete query drives Agent-selected evidence; no ticker list, phrase grammar, or service-side intent template may substitute for that loop. A relationship question requires exact quote/profile evidence for every named entity plus current Web/news/filing evidence for the relationship; a valuation question requires the actual inputs and periods used by its valuation frame. “Not found in this run” is not proof of nonexistence, so negative claims about ownership, customers, contracts, or other relationships require explicit evidence or must be phrased only as a bounded search result.
- Publication boundary: Interactive post-run reconstruction remains observational only. Completed Interactive bodies use a dedicated security-only finalizer that may remove system/internal protocol material, redact unsafe local paths, and stabilize local media references, but it may not rewrite market copy, veto planning-like prose, reconstruct an answer from tool outcomes, or invoke the scheduled/heartbeat completeness guard. The exact finalized body emitted to the user is persisted byte-for-byte. The service owns structural stream/session safety but may not semantically approve, prepend, normalize, retry, repair, reject, replace, or append a fixed “research incomplete/cannot safely publish” answer. Typed scheduled/heartbeat contracts keep their separate deterministic validation and fail-closed path.
- Provider and terminal boundary: The strict requested/effective tool-choice telemetry, typed finish plus internal DONE validation, adapter-local MiniMax clean-EOF normalization, current-run-only terminal evidence, canonical first-line commit, exact suffix publication, one buffered terminal-only recovery, and honest partial failure remain unchanged.
- Migration and rollback: No durable-data migration is required. The ledger, completion-signal availability, and terminal transition are request-local. A safe fallback may disable early terminal streaming and return to whole-body defer, but it must not restore isolated control, implicit finish, speculative tool-round publication, evidence-ledger publication veto, or post-run semantic review.
- Verification: Focused regressions must prove the ledger distinguishes search-only, post-search quote/profile, crypto, broad-market, and Web-only routes without rejecting a complete DirectFinal; business tools and eligible finish share one completion; only a valid sole `finish_research({})` enters terminal; every complete DirectFinal uses no retry, terminal/second generation, or replacement; malformed/mixed/provider-error paths do not authorize terminal; relationship prompts request quote/profile plus Web/news/filing evidence; and Interactive success bypasses semantic rewrite/refusal/tool-result reconstruction while visible and persisted bodies remain identical. Full gates, exact-commit build, zero-active-chat restart, health checks, and fresh CRWV/NVIDIA plus non-finance production canaries remain required. See `docs/adr/0004-agent-owned-research-loop.md`.

## D-2026-07-19-02 Share One Exact Last-Mile Evidence Contract Without A Publication Gate

- Status: Accepted; production acceptance is `in_progress`.
- Refines: `D-2026-07-19-01` and `docs/adr/0004-agent-owned-research-loop.md`. It does not restore any service-side Interactive response validator, rewrite, repair, refusal, or second generation.
- Refined by: `D-2026-07-19-04`; the exact terminal reminder is now owned by the normal tool-free completion, while DirectFinal remains only an unchanged provider compatibility/no-veto fallback.
- Superseded in production by: `D-2026-07-19-08` for the finish/terminal split. Its Session-time, quote-source-time, Web-snippet, and no-publication-gate constraints remain active in the natural DirectFinal prompt.
- Incident evidence: The first `0cd03abb` production replay produced one clean, byte-consistent answer in about 42.4 seconds with healthy DataFetch and Tavily calls, but no profile lookup. A Web snippet alone unlocked DirectFinal; the answer then used the quote-provider time as the query time and expanded snippets into unsupported customer-rank and supply-priority claims. The remaining defect was an asymmetric last mile, not stale deployment, frontend replay, or provider outage.
- Advisory evidence structure: After `data_fetch(search)`, the Agent prompt directs exact quote plus profile/asset-route attempts and the ledger records whether every batched quote symbol has matching profile/snapshot coverage. Crypto and broad-market paths have explicit diagnostic routes; genuine no-coverage is recorded and disclosed. This structure controls neither DirectFinal acceptance nor publication and adds no query phrase, company symbol, or relationship classifier.
- Shared final contract: DirectFinal and explicit terminal synthesis receive the same evidence-limited contract as the last ephemeral user message. Both get the exact literal first-line prefix generated for this turn. One live Beijing clock read supplies both `Session 上下文` and `answer_time_beijing`; quote provider timestamps belong only in `行情口径` and may never replace the Session query time.
- Evidence boundary: Post-DataFetch active requests start at the current-turn boundary, excluding stale prior ticker/price history. Assistant prose beside tool calls is blanked. Provider reasoning fields needed for MiniMax/Mimo tool-followup compatibility remain protocol context but are explicitly non-evidence; the tool-free terminal additionally strips them. The completed Interactive body still publishes and persists unchanged through the security-only finalizer.
- Source discipline: `web_search` accurately advertises basic, low-bandwidth results—at most three titles, URLs, and snippets, with no page body. Snippets and relationship labels may be used only literally and may not be expanded into unstated rights/duties, ranking, importance, exclusivity, guarantees, priority, concentration, or roles. The Agent should continue toward company announcements, filings, or other primary sources when those details matter.
- Verification: Regression must use distinct Session and quote timestamps; prove DirectFinal/terminal share the literal prefix and evidence contract; distinguish Web-only, quote-only, partial-profile, crypto, and broad-market traces without turning them into a DirectFinal veto; preserve provider reasoning replay for live tool follow-up; and prove one answer, no retry/terminal/second generation for DirectFinal, no reset/error/fixed refusal, and exact visible/persisted bytes. Full gates, exact-commit build, zero-active-chat restart, health checks, and fresh relationship/valuation/non-finance production canaries remain required.

## D-2026-07-19-03 Require Per-Entity Route Coverage Before Explicit Finish

- Status: Accepted; local focused verification passed, final deployment and production acceptance are `in_progress`.
- Refines: `D-2026-07-19-01`, `D-2026-07-19-02`, and `docs/adr/0004-agent-owned-research-loop.md`. It does not alter the rule that every complete natural DirectFinal is accepted unchanged and it does not restore an Interactive publication validator, refusal, repair, rewrite, or second generation.
- Refined by: `D-2026-07-19-04`; active tool-enabled prompts no longer invite DirectFinal and the relationship deletion reminder is replaced by terminal sentence-level claim provenance, without changing the no-veto fallback in code.
- Refined by: `D-2026-07-19-08`; route coverage now guides ordinary `Required → Auto` business-tool progression before natural DirectFinal and no longer exposes an explicit finish schema.
- Incident evidence: Deployed `c7c5741f` completed `crwv和英伟达有什么关系` as one clean answer in about 48.54 seconds after healthy DataFetch/Web calls, but quote/profile coverage existed only for CRWV. The structural ledger examined symbols that had already been quoted instead of every successful identity search route, so it exposed `finish_research` while NVIDIA still lacked a quote/profile attempt. Terminal prose then asserted largest/one-of-largest, core-customer, no-equity, preferred-partner, chip-model, contract-size, and party-role details beyond the available snippets. This was a pre-finish evidence-routing and Agent synthesis defect, not an FMP outage or proof that server-side semantic interception is needed.
- Per-route identity floor: The same Agent that reads the complete query assigns every named security a stable, distinct, case-sensitive `entity_route`, gives every individual search a call-scoped `identity_match=exact_symbol|name_or_alias`, and reuses the route verbatim across refinement, quote, profile/snapshot, and later DataFetch calls. A malformed later search cannot borrow an older route's valid match declaration. Any parseable DataFetch call carrying an explicit route immediately registers that route as pending, so it cannot disappear behind another route's coverage; it cannot preload candidates, quote/profile, or post evidence before its own valid search and result. Before explicit finish is available, each nonempty route needs one same candidate symbol in both its route-bound quote and profile/asset-route evidence. Coverage of CRWV cannot stand in for NVDA; quote/profile for CWY cannot satisfy an exact CRWV route; and an untagged symbol is attributed only when it belongs to exactly one active route.
- No natural-language entity parser: The service does not split query text on commas, `and`, `和`, `&`, `/`, or share-class wording, does not infer match mode from case/length/ticker shape, and does not positionally assign leftover provider rows. Legal names and codes such as AT&T, S&P Global, M&T Bank, H&R Block, BRK/B, and `Berkshire Hathaway, Class B` therefore remain one Agent-understood entity unless the Agent declares more. The first Agent-declared exact-symbol search fixes a persistent same-code constraint; the shared audited `hone-core::provider_symbol` equivalence handles only bounded share-class, exchange-suffix, Hong Kong padding, index, crypto-pair, and cashtag dialects. A later name refinement cannot widen CRWV to an ETF/product whose name embeds that code. Agent-declared name mode uses word-boundary grounding so Ford/FORD, Apple/Appleseed, and Tesla/derivative results do not collapse. Legacy untagged searches remain provisional active routes until an explicit search names one exact, case-sensitive old text through the actual `query`, `refines_query`, or `supersedes_query`. `refines_query` and `supersedes_query` are strictly mutually exclusive; a dual-link or mistyped metadata call migrates nothing and leaves its declared route pending. A valid single-source migration never guesses aliases and carries only search/empty attempt history, verbatim aliases, and an already Agent-declared exact-symbol constraint; it never imports provisional candidates, quote symbols, profile/asset-route symbols, or post-identity attempts. A route empty after two current consecutive unsuccessful searches additionally needs a real route-correct attempt after the latest empty result before bounded gap disclosure, while an unscoped no-symbol call is attributable only when there is one active route. Agent omission or reuse of a route remains an explicit Agent trust boundary and never authorizes a post-generation refusal.
- Control boundary: The structural ledger controls only `finish_research` schema visibility and the transition from business-tool `Required` guidance to business-tools-plus-finish `Auto`. It is evaluated before explicit finish, not after answer generation. It is never consulted to accept, reject, retry, suppress, replace, or persist a complete `Stop + Done` DirectFinal, and `AgentSession` still publishes a successful Interactive body through the security-only finalizer without semantic review.
- Relationship deletion check: The final ephemeral prompt for evidence-pending DirectFinal, structurally eligible DirectFinal, and explicit terminal synthesis instructs the Agent to delete every relationship claim not directly stated by current-turn source text or snippets. Titles, generic cooperation wording, purchase facts, and model memory cannot establish rank, preferred/core-customer status, equity ownership or its absence, cross-holdings, exclusivity, guarantees/priority, concentration, chip models, contract values, or party roles. This is Agent last-mile self-review, never a service-side keyword filter, validator, rewrite, or fixed refusal.
- Verification: Focused verification after the final route-order and migration changes is in progress. The regression matrix covers CRWV-only-CWY wrong-product output, CRWV/NVIDIA overlapping/noisy result sets, call-scoped match declarations, Ford/FORD, Apple/Appleseed, Tesla/derivative, shared provider aliases, same-route Chinese-name refinement replacement, mixed explicit/implicit routes without global provisional-route retirement, exact-text `supersedes_query` migration, failed exact/name migration without candidate inheritance, route evidence arriving before that route's search, AT&T/S&P/M&T/H&R/BRK/B/Class B preservation without a query splitter, singleton-only unscoped empty-route attribution, same-symbol quote/profile intersection, and the shared relationship deletion reminder while retaining no-veto DirectFinal behavior. Full repository gates, exact-commit build, graceful restart, health checks, and fresh relationship/relationship-plus-valuation/non-finance production canaries remain required; no deployment or production acceptance is claimed by this decision.

## D-2026-07-19-04 Make Tool-Enabled Research Rounds Evidence-Only

- Status: Accepted; local verification passed, exact deployment and production acceptance are `in_progress`.
- Refines: `D-2026-07-19-02`, `D-2026-07-19-03`, and `docs/adr/0004-agent-owned-research-loop.md`. It adds no service-side answer parser, publication gate, rewrite, retry, or fixed refusal.
- Refined by: `D-2026-07-19-05`; evidence-only rounds now end with a tolerant structured provenance handoff rather than an empty finish object, without changing the DirectFinal no-veto fallback or publication boundary.
- Superseded in production by: `D-2026-07-19-08` for the finish-only handoff and separate terminal. Tool-round buffering, concrete route guidance, bounded Web-snippet semantics, and evidence-limited natural prose remain active.
- Incident evidence: Exact production build `ac2468f9` removed the old completeness refusal and streamed `crwv和英伟达有什么关系` once in 46.300993 seconds with one start/delta/successful finish, no reset/error, byte-identical history, and zero remaining active chats. FMP and Tavily were healthy. The Agent nevertheless stopped after CRWV search+quote, NVIDIA search, and one Tavily query; it skipped CRWV profile and both NVDA quote/profile, then returned a tool-round DirectFinal that expanded snippets into unsupported largest/core-customer, MFN-priority, chip-model, and Forward-PE claims. The remaining defect was contradictory Agent guidance and weak snippet provenance, not deployment staleness, FMP failure, or a reason to intercept the answer afterward.
- Evidence-only rounds: The initial Agent instruction requires rereading the complete user query and, when companies/securities are named, returning only a separate DataFetch search call for every named entity with its own stable `entity_route` and call-scoped `identity_match`. After DataFetch activates finance, a `Required` round with incomplete structural coverage returns only real business-tool calls. Once the floor is satisfied, the `Auto` round returns either more business-tool calls or the sole `finish_research({})`; it does not write data time, summary, draft, explanation, or final prose. The following no-tool completion is the normal single visible answer. A provider may still ignore this protocol and return a complete `Stop + Done` DirectFinal; code accepts it unchanged as a compatibility/no-veto fallback and never converts it into an error or second generation.
- Dynamic route guidance: The request-local ledger now renders concrete per-route missing work: an undeclared/provisional route, a missing call-scoped search, current candidates, missing same-candidate quote or profile/snapshot/crypto quote, a mismatched quote/profile pair, or bounded no-coverage attempts. Route keys and candidate arrays are JSON-escaped and internal prefixes are not echoed. The status explicitly describes calls as attempted; it does not certify provider success, entity-set completeness, or semantic evidence quality, so the Agent must read each actual tool result and the original query before finishing.
- Snippet provenance: Successful `web_search` output is locally capped again to three results and receives a trusted root `hone_search_contract` plus per-result `hone_evidence`. The envelope states basic snippet-only scope, no page body, same-result inline citation scope, non-citable missing URLs, and that search order/score is not real-world rank; locally written metadata overwrites provider-spoofed names. It remains context for the Agent, not an output validator.
- Terminal claim ledger: The normal no-tool terminal requires every external relationship fact to point to the same current result whose title/content/snippet directly states its numbers, direction, rank, role, rights/obligations, product model, or valuation label, with source title and original URL inline beside the fact. A URL only locates evidence. Any judgment beyond literal source text must be a separate sentence beginning `推断：`; unsupported details are deleted. Missing evidence narrows the answer and is disclosed, but never becomes a whole-answer refusal.
- Verification: Agent 74/74, hone-tools 143 passed with one optional `matplotlib` test ignored, hone-channels 642/642, finance contracts 39/39, Web 267/267, the full workspace check/test commands, formatting/diff checks, and the complete CI-safe runner pass. The first full workspace test run caught `soul.md` exceeding the reviewed 12,000-character prompt budget; wording was compressed without removing the full prompt contract, the focused budget test passed, and the full workspace rerun then passed. Exact commit/build/restart, health checks, and fresh relationship/valuation/non-finance production canaries remain required before production acceptance is claimed.

## D-2026-07-19-05 Carry Current-Turn Provenance In finish_research

- Status: Historical; the Interactive production handoff/terminal protocol is superseded by `D-2026-07-19-08`.
- Refines: `D-2026-07-19-01`, `D-2026-07-19-04`, and `docs/adr/0004-agent-owned-research-loop.md`. It replaces only the empty-object finish payload and raw-transcript terminal boundary. Same-Agent ownership, evidence-only tool rounds, the unchanged DirectFinal compatibility fallback, one-output streaming, security-only Interactive finalization, and the ban on post-run semantic review remain active.
- Refined by: `D-2026-07-19-07`; current-turn citable sources are now bound into the finish schema, and an unresolved source locator receives at most one hidden same-Agent correction before terminal synthesis instead of authorizing an unrestricted empty-evidence terminal.
- Incident evidence: Exact production build `3d2474b5` returned `crwv和英伟达有什么关系` as one clean run and one byte-identical persisted answer with no reset, error, fixed refusal, or second generation. FMP and Tavily were healthy. The Agent nevertheless issued an empty finish after one broad relationship search, and terminal synthesis expanded three basic snippets into unsupported customer rank, GPU models, bargaining power, and “no equity relationship”. The terminal had the raw tool transcript, so the defect was not missing context; an empty completion signal did not force the same Agent to identify which current-turn evidence it intended to use.
- Structured handoff: Once the structural route floor is satisfied, a logical finish-only `finish_research` round carries `answer_scope`, `facts`, `inferences`, and `gaps`. The prompt asks for one control call, but provider-duplicated calls in the same finish-only round are tolerated: the runtime selects the first parseable handoff. If none validate, the terminal may use only the first JSON-readable nonempty scope recovered from fixed `facts[*].evidence[*]` paths; syntax-broken or truncated controls contribute no evidence and never invoke another research-model round. Each fact contains only a stable id and current-turn evidence references—there is no free-text fact claim. A Web reference identifies the current `tool_call_id`, 1-based result, and verbatim excerpt; the runtime injects the matched result's title and exact original URL. A DataFetch reference identifies the current `tool_call_id` plus a non-root scalar RFC 6901 JSON Pointer. Inferences cite submitted fact ids; attempted but unresolved dimensions stay in gaps. An inference remains Agent-authored judgment rather than a service-certified fact.
- Tolerant mechanical resolution: The runtime resolves every reference independently by identity, index/pointer, exact text, URL, and bounded-size rules. A stale, fabricated, missing, or malformed individual reference is omitted or annotated; it cannot reject the whole handoff, send the Agent through another completion loop, surface an internal protocol error, or authorize a fixed refusal. Valid references and facts in the same submission remain usable. Compact fallback is confined to the Agent-selected locator: one Web result number or the exact direct DataFetch object around a selected scalar/missing leaf. `data_fetch(search)`, missing current-turn invocations, unselected same-turn tool results, adjacent batched entities or periods, root/array/null selections, and `error/errors/isError/*_error` paths cannot supply terminal evidence. Duplicated invalid controls never union scopes, and a gaps-only handoff imports nothing. Compact fallback is deterministic evidence transport, not relevance ranking, claim validation, or answer composition.
- Terminal isolation: The tool-free terminal is reconstructed from the current system contract, current user request, exact Session-time anchor, and resolved handoff evidence/inferences/gaps only. It receives no raw business-tool transcript, prior assistant/tool messages, earlier user evidence, discarded drafts, or provider reasoning. The terminal derives factual wording directly from resolved excerpts/fields. Missing evidence may narrow or qualify the answer; it must never become a service-owned whole-answer ban.
- Publication boundary: Hone performs protocol and source-location mechanics before terminal synthesis and security/path cleanup afterward. It never semantically approves a proposition, parses or scores final prose, retries because of answer content, rewrites a conclusion, appends a completeness message, or refuses an otherwise successful Interactive answer. A provider that ignores the tool-only protocol and returns complete `Stop + Done` prose remains an unchanged DirectFinal with no terminal, second generation, or veto.
- Control and failure boundary: Mixed finish plus real business calls continues only the real calls and ignores every accompanying finish control. A finish-only round with one or more provider-duplicated controls enters terminal once through its first parseable handoff or, when none validate, the first safely readable fixed-path evidence scope; it never scans or unions the whole current turn. These control calls never enter actor registry, tool budget, progress, observer, audit body, or persistence, and neither duplication nor malformed handoff starts another research loop. Genuine provider error, timeout, incomplete lifecycle, or iteration exhaustion remains a failed run; tolerant evidence resolution does not disguise those transport/runtime failures as success.
- Verification: Regressions cover partial success in one handoff; stale tool ids; wrong result indices, excerpts, and JSON Pointers; multiple valid and invalid references together; no free-text fact claim or model-supplied URL; runtime title/URL injection; inference references; selected-result/object fallback; exclusion of search candidates, missing invocations, unselected NBIS/CWY results, neighboring batched symbols/periods, arrays, nulls, roots, and error paths; syntax-broken empty scope; provider-duplicated controls without scope union or another research loop; mixed business/finish behavior; no-success gaps; terminal messages containing only the current system contract, current user request, and resolved/scoped-fallback evidence; absence of every older conversational turn, raw tool transcript, and reasoning; no invalid-reference loop or public error; DirectFinal no-veto behavior; and one exact visible/persisted body. Focused Agent tests pass 96/96 and finance contracts pass 39/39. Exact-commit build, zero-active-chat graceful restart, health checks, and fresh relationship/valuation/non-finance canaries remain required.
- Migration and rollback: No durable-data migration is required. The handoff and compact evidence are request-local. A safe rollback may disable early terminal streaming and defer the whole Agent body, but it must not restore the empty-object terminal handoff, raw-transcript evidence ambiguity, post-run semantic validation, answer rewriting, or fixed refusal.

## D-2026-07-19-06 Retry Only Idempotent Web Requests And Recover Chat Streams Before Error

- Status: Accepted; local implementation verification passed, exact deployment and production acceptance are `in_progress`.
- Refines: The public-chat run ownership and one-terminal rules in `docs/invariants.md` and `docs/repo-map.md`. It changes browser transport behavior only; backend run registration, quota, SSE wire events, and Agent answer semantics remain unchanged.
- Incident evidence: The shared Web `apiFetch` helper retried every method after a transient 408/502/503/504 response or transport exception. A retry of `POST /api/public/chat` could create a second submission after the first request had already reached the server. Separately, the chat catch path first changed the assistant card to error and only afterward called bootstrap/history recovery, producing the reported brief error flash even when the authoritative run was still active.
- Retry boundary: Automatic browser retry is permitted only for idempotent `GET` and `HEAD`. `POST /api/public/chat` and every other non-idempotent method are submitted exactly once at the browser helper layer, regardless of transient status or transport exception. Recovery observes server state; it never repeats the user operation.
- Stream recovery boundary: A non-Abort SSE interruption keeps the existing assistant card pending with a recovery status, then uses the normal bootstrap/history retry policy to discover the authoritative `active_run`, completed answer, or interrupted state. Only exhaustion of that recovery path renders an error. An explicit user abort/stop closes immediately and does not enter disconnect recovery.
- Run identity and time: Refresh and recovery retain the server-owned `run_id` and original start time. They must not create a new local thinking epoch, replay POST, synthesize a second answer, or accept frames after unique terminal `run_finished`.
- Restore/send ordering: Every bootstrap restore captures both a sync generation and the current local-send generation. A new send synchronously advances both fences, aborts the prior restore, and cancels its retry timer before appending the optimistic user/assistant pair. An older bootstrap therefore cannot reconcile history over the next turn and delete the assistant card before its first delta arrives.
- Verification: Frontend tests prove GET/HEAD transient and transport retry, POST transient and transport one-shot behavior, public-chat single submission, non-Abort recovery-first state, explicit abort immediate closure, error only after recovery exhaustion, original server elapsed time after refresh, ignoring late frames after terminal, and rejection of a previous-turn restore after a newer local send. Web tests pass 274/274 and typecheck/build pass. Production acceptance still requires one user row, one assistant row, one public terminal, no duplicate active run, no lost first delta, and no transient error flash during a controlled stream-disconnect recovery.
- Migration and rollback: No server or durable-data migration is required. Rolling back the method guard or recovery-first ordering would reintroduce duplicate-run and flicker risk and is not a safe production workaround.

## D-2026-07-19-07 Bind finish_research To The Current-Turn Source Catalog

- Status: Historical; the Interactive production source-catalog/correction/terminal protocol is superseded by `D-2026-07-19-08`.
- Refines: `D-2026-07-19-05` and `docs/adr/0004-agent-owned-research-loop.md`. It changes only the pre-terminal source-location protocol. Same-Agent evidence ownership, evidence-only tool rounds, the unchanged DirectFinal compatibility fallback, one-output streaming, security-only Interactive finalization, and the ban on post-answer semantic review, rewrite, refusal, or publication veto remain active.
- Incident evidence: Exact build `ae4d9a8f` passed repository gates, was built with its immutable prompt/skill snapshot, and was gracefully deployed from the repository root with cloud mode, Postgres, object storage, ports, auth boundaries, and enabled channels healthy. A fresh direct actor that omitted `channel_scope` then asked `crwv和英伟达有什么关系`. The run had one start, one visible answer, one successful finish, no reset/error/second answer, byte-identical history, zero remaining active chats, and first/final visible output at about 101.748 seconds. FMP DataFetch and Tavily Web calls were healthy. Content still failed: the finish handoff used generic strings such as `web_search`, `quote`, and `profile` instead of the real opaque current-turn call IDs. The ae4 runtime did not recover the generic Web ID, the DataFetch locators were also malformed, every submitted reference was dropped, and terminal synthesis received `facts=[]`; it then reversed the disclosed capacity-purchase direction and added unsupported customer/supplier, investment, GPU-model, allocation, and ranking claims. This was a source-binding defect inside the Agent loop, not stale deployment, FMP/Tavily failure, frontend replay, or evidence that an answer interceptor is needed.
- Runtime source catalog: Before exposing `finish_research`, the runtime derives a bounded catalog only from matching current-turn invocation/result pairs. It includes successful non-search DataFetch results with usable scalar data and successful Web results that have an original URL plus excerpt-capable text. DataFetch search, failed results, results without a matching invocation, unsupported tools, empty data, and non-citable Web rows are excluded. The active prompt shows only exact opaque IDs plus structural descriptions, never source excerpts, URLs, errors, or a whole-turn evidence dump.
- Dynamic exact-ID schema: When the catalog is nonempty, every `facts[*].evidence[*].tool_call_id` receives an enum containing exactly those current-turn citable IDs; tool names and DataFetch data types are explicitly not IDs. An empty catalog does not emit a provider-hostile empty enum. The evidence schema stays flat for OpenAI-compatible provider support, while the prompt keeps Web fields (`result_number + exact_excerpt`) and DataFetch fields (`json_pointer`) mutually exclusive.
- Mechanical resolution and correction: Exact IDs remain authoritative. For a provider that substitutes a generic Web tool name, the runtime may recover the locator only when the submitted 1-based result number and verbatim excerpt identify exactly one current-turn Web invocation; zero or multiple matches authorize nothing. DataFetch generic names are never guessed. If the current turn has citable catalog entries but the handoff contains an unresolved locator or contains neither a mechanically resolved related fact nor scoped fallback, the same Agent receives one hidden, pre-terminal, locator-only correction containing the exact catalog. This is not a second research role, business-tool replay, answer-content review, post-generation repair, or visible response rewrite. Valid sibling facts survive mechanical validation and may continue to terminal after that bounded correction.
- Failure and publication boundary: A direct gaps-only handoff is legal only when the current-turn citable catalog is empty, meaning the real attempted sources supplied no citable result; it must disclose concrete gaps and imports no evidence. When the catalog is nonempty, gaps alone never authorize terminal: after the one correction, the handoff must retain at least one mechanically resolved related fact or scoped fallback. Remaining invalid siblings may then be omitted and terminal uses only the valid evidence. If neither survives, the run fails as a genuine finish-protocol failure and never enters an unrestricted empty-evidence terminal. Syntax-broken controls still have no implicit whole-turn evidence authority; with citable sources they receive the same single correction, otherwise they fail rather than fabricate. Mixed business/finish rounds continue only the business calls, and provider-duplicated finish controls retain first-parseable/fixed-scope handling without scope union. A complete nonempty `Stop + Done` DirectFinal still publishes unchanged with no terminal, retry, semantic approval, rejection, replacement, or second generation.
- Verification: Focused regressions cover citable-source enumeration, exclusion of search/failure/missing-invocation/unsupported sources, dynamic exact-ID enums, an empty-catalog schema without an empty enum, unique generic-Web excerpt recovery and ambiguous rejection, generic finish IDs corrected before one grounded terminal, malformed finish receiving one hidden same-Agent locator correction, valid sibling evidence, direct gaps-only only for an empty catalog, nonempty-catalog gaps-only correction/failure, current-turn terminal isolation, and unchanged DirectFinal no-veto behavior. `hone-agent` passes 101/101, `hone-channels` passes 642/642, and finance static contracts pass 39/39. Full repository gates, exact-commit rebuild/restart, health checks, and fresh relationship/valuation/non-finance production canaries remain required before acceptance.
- Migration and rollback: No durable-data migration is required; the catalog, enum, correction counter, and resolved handoff are request-local. A safe rollback must not restore raw-transcript terminal authority, unrestricted empty-evidence synthesis, post-answer semantic validation, answer rewriting, fixed refusal, or browser POST replay.

## D-2026-07-19-08 Use The Standard Agent Loop And Natural DirectFinal For Interactive Finance

- Status: Accepted; local implementation and full proportional verification are complete, while exact deployment and production acceptance remain pending.
- Supersedes: The Interactive production use of `finish_research`, structured handoff/source catalog, locator-only correction, tool-free terminal synthesis/recovery, and terminal semantic audit in `D-2026-07-18-03` and `D-2026-07-19-01` through `D-2026-07-19-07`. It preserves their current-turn entity/evidence ownership, route-aware structural ledger, provider lifecycle/deadline safety, speculative tool-round buffering, one-output publication, exact visible/persisted bytes, Web POST one-shot recovery, and typed scheduled/heartbeat boundary.
- Incident evidence: Exact production build `d4d44735` executed ten DataFetch/Web calls successfully for `crwv和英伟达有什么关系`; FMP, DataFetch, and Tavily were healthy. The request still used seven model calls and took 92.435 seconds. Business tools consumed only about 8.9 seconds, while the post-evidence finish locator, locator correction, and tool-free terminal consumed about 13.993, 19.925, and 19.363 seconds respectively. The terminal then inferred “纽交所收盘价” from `market_date_new_york` despite NASDAQ entity evidence and invented unsupported core-supplier/major-customer/high-dependence/lock/multiple-binding claims from an empty inference set. The defect was the service-imposed completion pipeline, not stale deployment or a provider outage.
- Agent ownership: Production Interactive finance uses one ordinary function-calling Agent loop, one context, and one model role. The Agent reads the full query, identifies every named company/security, loads relevant DataFetch/Web/business-tool results into that same context, and then either calls another real tool or returns one natural nonempty `Stop + Done` DirectFinal. The same Agent that selects evidence writes the user answer.
- Production prohibition: The production Interactive tool schema does not expose `finish_research`. The service does not request a completion handoff, correct opaque locators, start a tool-free second generation, run a terminal audit, semantically approve/reject final prose, rewrite market copy, append a completeness warning, or replace the answer with a fixed refusal. The legacy builder/state is compiled only for tests, so a production binary cannot enable it.
- Channel and history boundary: `AgentRunnerRequest.agent_owned_finance_loop` selects this mode independently of terminal streaming policy for Interactive `function_calling` turns on Web, Feishu, Discord, and Telegram. Finance requests use the composed current input plus at most four prior user utterances / 4,000 characters, explicitly labelled as reference-only context. Historical assistant/tool/reasoning content, prices, financials, and conclusions are excluded. A compact summary may already exist inside the composed current input, but neither source is current-turn fact evidence or authority to add an unreferenced entity.
- Structural guidance: Entity routes, exact/name match declarations, identity-only search semantics, bounded no-coverage attempts, and quote/profile/asset-route coverage remain request-local guidance and telemetry. Before the evidence floor, tool choice is business-tool `Required`; after it, the same real tools remain under `Auto`, allowing more evidence work or natural DirectFinal. Lowercase/mixed-case symbols in a securities context still take normalized exact-symbol lookup. The floor is never a publication gate and cannot veto a complete answer.
- Output boundary: Tool-capable rounds remain buffered because their prose may precede another tool call. Only the completed natural DirectFinal becomes visible and persisted, once, with no reset or second answer. Provider/transport/deadline/lifecycle failures remain genuine failures and cannot manufacture finish authority or investment-completeness copy.
- Time and exchange evidence: Security/market/sector answers still begin with current Session Beijing query time and an evidence-based quote basis. Quote-source time should come from `hone_quote_time.beijing`. `market_date_new_york` and generic `new_york` values establish only a market date/timezone; they do not prove NYSE, a close, or regular-session close. Exchange wording must come from structured `exchange` / `exchangeShortName` fields.
- Relationship language: Bounded Web snippets are not page bodies or rankings. Current evidence must directly support relationship direction and strength. When it does not, the Agent uses neutral wording and must not elevate it to core/largest/major customer or supplier, high dependence, locked/exclusive/priority status, multiple binding, ownership/non-ownership, GPU model, contract value, or similar detail.
- Typed boundary: Caller-supplied scheduled/heartbeat investment contracts keep their deterministic preparation, validation, fallback, and fail-closed path. This decision changes only open-ended Interactive finance.
- Verification: Production-path tests expose only real business tools, prove `Required → Auto` around the structural floor, accept one natural DirectFinal with no finish/correction/terminal/audit model call, keep tool-round drafts hidden, and emit/persist one byte-identical body. They also cover channel-independent mode selection, bounded prior-user follow-up wording with historical assistant/tool/price exclusion, hallucinated retired and generic unregistered tool names using ordinary unknown-tool behavior without public tool status, and lowercase `query="crwv"` plus `identity_match="exact_symbol"` resolving canonical `CRWV`. The CRWV/NVIDIA fixture supplies entity-bound quote/profile fields and explicit relationship excerpts so its time, NASDAQ, purchase direction, investor, and MFN assertions cannot be prewritten without evidence. Content tests distinguish Session time, `hone_quote_time.beijing`, `market_date_new_york`, and NASDAQ exchange fields and reject unsupported relationship-strength expansion through Agent prompt behavior rather than a service output filter. The complete pre-rebase workspace test passed; exact rebased commit `e25a2879` passed workspace check, Agent 104/104, channels 643/643, Web 275/275 plus public build, finance contracts 39/39, all CI-safe regressions, formatting, and diff checks. Production acceptance still requires exact-commit build/deploy, zero-active-chat restart, PG/object-store/API/channel health, fresh relationship/valuation/single-ticker/non-finance canaries, no more than five model calls on the normal relationship path, under-60-second first-visible target, one public terminal, exact history, and zero active chats afterward.
- Migration and rollback: No durable-data migration is required. A safe fallback may keep the final natural body fully buffered; it must not reactivate finish handoff, locator correction, tool-free terminal generation, semantic review, fixed refusal, or browser POST replay.

## D-2026-07-19-09 Deliver Authenticated Community Archives From Private R2 At The Edge

- Status: Accepted; implementation and local verification are complete. The initial private production R2 snapshot was published and idempotently rechecked on 2026-07-19 (`662` contents, `833` resources, `754` publication objects, final `no_op=true`). The exact-route Worker is now deployed without a secret or enabling variable and returns the reviewed fail-closed `503`. Implementation commits `385e35b0` and `100f5608` are on `main`; automatic Pages deployment completed with discovery compiled out. Exact backend build `100f5608` is staged locally but has not replaced or restarted the previous process, so backend configuration and user traffic remain unchanged. The remaining actions stay explicit operator steps in `docs/runbooks/backend-deployment.md`.
- Context: The legacy `/api/public/community` path reads PostgreSQL on each feed request, while resource delivery authenticates, looks up metadata, downloads the complete object through the backend, and verifies it before responding. The backend remains the compatibility and personal-state authority, but putting every feed page, image, PDF, and attachment byte on that hot path makes origin latency and bandwidth the user-visible bottleneck. Adding Redis would not remove the binary proxy or provide the durable object delivery layer.
- Decision: PostgreSQL stays the canonical community archive and the existing private R2 bucket stays the durable resource store. A dry-run-first `hone-cli cloud community-publish` projects non-personal feed pages, immutable resource descriptors, and a mutable active-version index into the same private bucket. Apply reads through one repeatable-read/read-only snapshot on the dedicated PG advisory-lock session, fully hashes eligible resource objects before any publication write, rechecks lock liveness, refuses empty/conflicting/oversized output, writes descriptors then `resources/active.json` then cursor pages, and updates mutable `feed/latest.json` last. Mutable writes are read back and explicit unlock failure is fatal. Dry-run checks exact-key HEAD existence only and labels that weaker proof. Redis is deliberately excluded from the binary hot path; it may be considered later only for independently measured metadata or personalized-state contention.
- Authentication and cache boundary: The backend and Worker require the same trimmed 32..1024-byte HMAC secret. The backend issues a short-lived grant only through a scoped HttpOnly cookie and never returns the token, secret, or actor identity in JSON; invalid secret configuration disables and clears grants. The exact-route Worker validates that grant, then checks every resource id/version against uncached `active.json` before any shared-byte cache lookup, so a later revocation cannot be bypassed by cached bytes. R2 remains private with no `r2.dev` or bucket custom domain. Static feed/resource bytes may use a canonical shared cache after authentication, resource cache TTL is at most one hour, while the browser response remains private/Vary Cookie and actor-specific unread/seen state stays on the backend. Errors, inactive resources, invalid indexes/descriptors/integrity results, and legacy responses are not shared-cache entries.
- Compatibility boundary: The backend defaults to `community_delivery.mode=off`; `shadow` can exercise grant issuance without client cutover, and only `prefer` plus a separately enabled Pages build flag activates client discovery. The client validates the fixed base path and each versioned resource path, then immediately falls back to the unchanged legacy feed/resource API on discovery, GET, HEAD, image, PDF, or download failure. The Worker can fall back only on eligible feed/resource GET misses to the fixed legacy origin, requests identity encoding, enforces 8MiB/128MiB legacy response ceilings, and forwards only `hone_web_session`, never the edge or unrelated cookies. Resource HEAD failures and inactive/invalid/integrity states return non-2xx without an internal second download.
- Rollout and rollback: Roll out in layers: backend/Pages remain off; for a brand-new Worker confirm no remote `EDGE_DISABLED` value exists, while any existing/restored Worker must first deploy explicit `EDGE_DISABLED=true`; install the same secret at backend and Worker; publish and verify descriptors/active index/pages/latest; move backend to `shadow`; explicitly deploy `EDGE_DISABLED=false`; validate authenticated edge and origin fallback traffic; move backend to `prefer`; then deploy Pages with discovery enabled. Roll back first by setting `EDGE_DISABLED=true` in the dashboard and deploying (removing it is unreliable with `keep_vars=true`), then return the Pages flag to zero, and finally return the backend to `shadow` or `off` on the next operator-managed restart. Emergency resource revocation disables the Worker, performs a Cloudflare global purge rather than data-center-local `cache.delete`, publishes an active-index tombstone, and verifies the old direct path returns 404 before re-enabling. Existing grants expire within one hour and immutable R2 artifacts are retained for diagnosis/retry rather than deleted during rollback.
- Consequences: Feed and resource bodies can be served without a database lookup or full object relay on the normal edge hit, while current users retain the same routes and visible behavior during a staged cutover. The cache is Cloudflare Cache API locality, not a new source of truth or guaranteed tiered cache; R2 remains the durable fallback. Image transformation/CDN variants are intentionally deferred until origin-size and format measurements justify a separate Cloudflare Images binding and cost model.
- Verification: Required local proof covers Rust grant/config/logout behavior and the Rust-to-Worker golden token, publisher planning/locking/idempotence, Worker fail-closed/auth-before-cache/R2/fallback rules, and frontend discovery/path validation/immediate fallback. Production acceptance additionally requires the phased health, anonymous/authenticated, feed/resource, browser, monitoring, and rollback canaries in the deployment runbook.

## D-2026-07-21-01 Commit Only Delivered Interactive Prefixes And Remove Synchronous TTFT Work

- Status: Accepted; implementation and proportional local verification are required before exact deployment, and production latency acceptance remains pending.
- Refines: `D-2026-07-19-08` and `docs/adr/0004-agent-owned-research-loop.md`. The ordinary same-Agent research loop, Agent-owned natural DirectFinal, route/evidence ledger, speculative tool-round boundary, typed scheduled/heartbeat separation, and exact visible/persisted-byte contract remain active.
- Incident evidence: Production received `大A有没有类似CRWV、Nebius这样的数据中心的标的` at `2026-07-21 19:50:19.973` and completed at `19:51:59.427`, about `99.45s` later. Synchronous pre-run SessionCompress alone consumed `14.377s`; five model calls consumed about `76.5s`; ten business tools consumed about `8.45s`; and the final model stream spent about `39.45s` generating a complete answer that the runner buffered. Web SSE/client delivery was immediate after backend emission. The critical path was backend compaction, serial model/tool planning, and full-final buffering—not frontend rendering or a provider outage.
- Context path: Only a self-contained strict Interactive request whose current input contains a deterministic explicit security seed uses the fast first attempt. It skips pre-run compaction and compact-summary injection, obtains one durable Session snapshot, and derives both active context and at most four earlier user-only reference utterances from it, ordered by final durable user-row position and strictly before the current row. Historical automation, failed groups, slash commands, summaries, assistant bodies, tool payloads, and same-text collisions are excluded. Previously invoked skill prompts are restored separately from metadata, remain subject to current activation, and cannot be displaced by the user-reference budget. Referential follow-ups keep full history. Context overflow still force-compacts and retries with the new summary; manual `/compact` and noninteractive paths keep their existing behavior.
- Tool batching: The earliest model round asks for every independent operation in one tool-call batch: named-entity DataFetch searches plus ticker-independent Web/news/filing/industry candidate discovery. The executor remains serial; the batching change removes avoidable model round trips, not tool-await time. Quote/profile/snapshot/financials and other symbol-dependent tools wait for the corresponding route-bound search result. A name, abbreviation, model memory, or first semantic search result never authorizes ticker guessing.
- Streaming commit boundary: Before the structural evidence floor, every model-authored tool-capable delta remains speculative. On Web after the floor, an eligible natural final may still progressively commit a complete bounded canonical `数据时间：北京时间 …；行情口径：…` line and later byte-stable body lines. D-2026-07-22-01 additionally permits one typed service-owned neutral prefix to ACK before the first model call for deterministic explicit-security input, or after the safe deferred DataFetch activation boundary for other finance; it does not make model tool-round prose visible. Leading whitespace, preambles, Markdown, unsafe controls, oversized/incomplete headers, and prompt markers remain hidden.
- Delivered-state rule: Each header/body delta extends `committed_visible_prefix` only after the unique downstream publication sink's await succeeds. Cancellation, a rejected or closed SSE receiver, ambiguous sinks, observer failure, or backpressure before success records no bytes for that delta and cannot create phantom persisted content. Web first-delta telemetry and counters likewise advance only after the channel send succeeds.
- Side-effect boundary: D-2026-07-26-02 supersedes the earlier assumption that a tool call after a model-authored exact canonical header is automatically a protocol error. The exact line is an irreversible delivery boundary but only `Stop + Done` makes it terminal; a following batch may execute only when every tool is registered, every argument payload parses and is structurally valid, and every effect is known read-only. The entire batch otherwise fails before assistant framing, observer notification, registry execution, or network access. If later completion fails after the prefix was delivered, Session appends the fixed research-failure disclosure and persists exactly the visible prefix-plus-suffix as one partial terminal.
- Observability: Record distinct timestamps for the first provider delta, first successfully committed runner prefix, first successfully sent Web `assistant_delta`, and final lifecycle event. Attempted callbacks or failed sends are not evidence of user-visible output.
- Migration and rollback: No durable-data migration is required. A safe rollback may restore whole-final buffering, but it must not restore synchronous pre-run compaction for an eligible self-contained fast-path request, record a prefix before emitter success, execute a tool after commitment, replay persistent work, stream speculative preambles, or reactivate a second synthesis/publication gate.
- Verification: Unit and CI-safe coverage must include canonical-header/body recognition, byte-stable line progression, unsafe/prompt-marker fallback, cancelled/rejected/closed-emitter phantom-prefix prevention, post-ACK registered/structurally-valid/known-read-only admission and fail-closed rejection before execution, exact partial persistence, self-contained fast-path gating, one-snapshot reference and invoked-skill recovery across a compact boundary, historical same-text/automation/failed/slash exclusion, summary-load elision, and the exact CRWV/Nebius comparable-discovery batching prompt. Production acceptance requires an exact-commit immutable build, zero-active-chat SIGINT restart, cloud authority/PG/object-store/auth probes, one fresh actor with the exact incident query, first-visible and final timings, progressive deltas, one successful terminal, byte-identical two-row history, and zero active chats afterward.

## D-2026-07-22-01 Bound Interactive Finance Fan-out And ACK A Typed Web Prefix

- Status: Accepted; implementation and focused regressions are in progress. Exact immutable redeployment and production acceptance remain pending.
- Refines: `D-2026-07-19-08`, `D-2026-07-21-01`, and `docs/adr/0004-agent-owned-research-loop.md`. It keeps one Agent/context/model role, the absence of `finish_research` and a second synthesizer, current-turn evidence ownership, security-only finalization, and exact visible/persisted bytes. It replaces the assumptions that route growth can remain unbounded, that all tool calls must stop after any delivered prefix, and that the first visible prefix must be model-authored after the structural floor.
- Incident evidence: Exact commit `b06de76a` was built and deployed successfully, but a fresh direct Web canary for `大A有没有类似CRWV、Nebius这样的数据中心的标的` still produced no assistant delta within 240 seconds and completed in `246.037s`. Eleven model calls consumed `200.365s`; 105 tools consumed `45.271s` (`44` DataFetch searches, `42` quotes, `2` profiles, and `17` Web calls). The request-local ledger expanded from the two user anchors to `26` routes with `24` still unresolved, so the structural floor remained false and the provider stayed in `Required` tool planning. SSE transport emitted progress/tool events immediately and the final `3,731`-character assistant row persisted only at completion. The remaining bottleneck was unbounded same-Agent research fan-out, not SSE, the browser, or one slow tool.
- Typed prefix: A Web Interactive finance turn receives exactly one service-owned first line: `数据时间：北京时间 {Session answer time}；行情口径：本轮仅使用可核验资料，具体报价时间与数据缺口在正文逐项披露`. This line states time and evidence policy only; it contains no price, exchange, relationship, candidate, valuation, or completeness claim. The model's final must reproduce it byte-for-byte. A deterministic explicit-security fast path may request downstream ACK before the first model call. A name-only or otherwise deferred finance turn may request ACK only after a budget-accepted batch includes at least one call to the registered DataFetch tool with parseable arguments, a supported `data_type`, every required security target, and valid identity-search shape, while every call in that batch is registered, structurally valid, and known read-only. Unsupported/missing-target/malformed/rejected DataFetch never activates research or deferred ACK. Ordinary non-finance turns never ACK this line.
- Delivered-state and failure rule: The prefix becomes visible state only when the unique downstream sink accepts `emit_committed`; an attempted, rejected, cancelled, closed, or ambiguous delivery remains invisible. Once ACKed, every call in every later batch must name a tool in the active registry, have parseable structurally valid arguments (including DataFetch type/target/identity shape), and be classified known read-only. If any call fails that conjunction, the whole batch fails before assistant tool framing, observer notification, registry execution, or network access; an unknown read-only-looking alias is still unknown. If the Agent later fails or returns a final that cannot preserve the exact prefix, the public transcript appends `本轮研究未能完成，暂未形成可供参考的标的结论。`, persists those exact visible bytes with failure/partial metadata, and closes once without a transient error card. That suffix discloses failure and does not manufacture an investment answer.
- Bounded research: Finance mode has intrinsic request-local ceilings even when callers omit limits: at most three tool-call batches, 24 total tool calls, 20 DataFetch calls, six Web calls, and six accepted identity routes. An ACKed typed prefix starts finance accounting immediately; Web-only batches before DataFetch activation count against the three-batch, total-call, and Web-call ceilings and therefore also lead to the same-Agent `tools=[]` final. Only a valid identity search creates a route. If a search carries an explicit `entity_route`, that same call must contain `identity_match=exact_symbol|name_or_alias`; missing/invalid match mode is rejected before observer/registry/network access and cannot create or poison a route. The six-route ceiling is enforced during admission of the first batch as well as later batches. If an unknown alias returns a symbol that uniquely matches one accepted route, it merges into that route. The prompt reserves the route budget for user anchors first and normally no more than four screening candidates. Because the service deliberately does not infer which model-authored routes semantically came from the user's prose, the hard cap is admission-order based and cannot guarantee every anchor when more than six securities are requested; the final must state the resulting coverage gap.
- Natural bounded final: When the third finance tool batch or a tool budget is exhausted, the next iteration of the same `Agent::run` receives `tools=[]` and an explicit instruction to write one natural final only from existing current-turn results, with concrete gaps for missing coverage. This is neither the retired `finish_research` protocol nor a handoff, locator correction, terminal audit, recovery model, or second generation: no new role/context is created, and the same Agent continues from its existing transcript. A provider that emits another tool call in that tools-disabled iteration fails the run.
- Consequences and rollback: The exact-seed Web path can show an honest stable first line before model latency, while name-only finance cannot masquerade as research until a real safe DataFetch batch has run. Research cost and latency are bounded independently of prompt compliance, and the body remains Agent-authored. A safe rollback may disable the early prefix or lower the existing budgets, but must not reintroduce unbounded route admission, claim an un-ACKed prefix as visible, exempt post-ACK Web-only work from ceilings, allow an unregistered/invalid/non-read-only call after ACK, restore a second terminal generator, or persist bytes different from the transcript.
- Verification: Agent regressions must cover the three-batch `tools=[]` transition including Web-only batches after T0 ACK, intrinsic total/DataFetch/Web ceilings without caller budgets, first-batch six-route admission, explicit-route missing/invalid `identity_match` rejection before network with no ledger pollution, unique-symbol alias merge, unsupported/missing-target/budget-rejected DataFetch that cannot activate deferred ACK, safe registered read-only tools after ACK, and pre-frame/observer/registry/network rejection of unregistered/invalid/non-read-only batches. Runner/Session regressions must cover pre-model and deferred ACK, rejection/cancellation, ordinary non-finance isolation, exact prefix-plus-tail success, exact prefix-plus-failure-suffix partial persistence, and one terminal with visible bytes equal to the assistant row. Production acceptance requires an exact pushed revision and immutable build, zero-active-chat restart, health/auth/storage/channel checks, then a fresh actor replay of the incident query with first `assistant_delta` under 60 seconds (target under five seconds for the deterministic seed), no more than four model calls, no more than 24 tools and six routes, one terminal, no reset/error flash, byte-identical visible/history content, and active chats returning to zero.

## D-2026-07-26-01 Preserve The Requested Session In Interactive Market-Move Explanations

- Status: Accepted; the first exact immutable deployment exposed an adjacent canonical-header continuation defect, whose focused repair passed local verification; replacement deployment and production canaries remain required.
- Refines: `D-2026-07-19-08`, `D-2026-07-21-01`, `D-2026-07-22-01`, and `docs/adr/0004-agent-owned-research-loop.md`. It adds request-local evidence-ordering guidance, not a server-side answer parser, historical-price engine, semantic publication gate, rewrite, retry, or second generation.
- Incident evidence: Web direct `美股股价下跌原因` twice completed current benchmark quotes and Web searches but the production `2563f7ad` runtime replaced the result with the generic committed-prefix research failure; main already contained later prefix recovery that production had not deployed. Feishu direct `周五暴跌` then searched `2026-07-25`, `2026-07-24`, and `2026-07-23` in succession, reported Friday broad-market moves as limited, and silently redirected the requested Friday explanation to a larger Thursday move. The date/weekday pair `2026-07-23 周四` was itself correct; the defect was target-session and scope substitution, not calendar arithmetic for that pair.
- Target-session ordering: For a time-sensitive move question, the same Agent first identifies the requested instrument/market scope and target interval, then verifies whether that exact object moved as claimed in that interval, and only then explains causes. Explicit dates, weekdays, and session labels have priority. A latest quote, current civil date, different session's larger move, broad index, sector, and single stock cannot substitute for one another. If broad benchmarks do not support the user's premise, the answer states the scope mismatch and checks the named sector/security or asks one minimal clarification; it does not select another day to make the premise true.
- Calendar hint boundary: The current turn may receive a deterministic Beijing and relevant market-local civil-calendar table plus the nearest past/current same-weekday candidate. This hint is derived from the same Session clock used by the answer prefix. It proves only civil dates and weekdays; it does not prove an exchange was open, a holiday/half-day rule, a market session, a close, or a price move. When the current text does not name a market, the default New York table is explicitly labeled only as a US-market candidate and cannot itself establish scope.
- Evidence boundary: A latest quote percentage belongs only to its provider timestamp and cannot establish a different historical session. Market-move causes require current-turn Web/news/announcement text that covers the same object and same absolute market-local date. If that evidence remains insufficient, the Agent still returns the verified move/scope and the explicit gap `原因本轮未完全核验`; it must not manufacture a trigger or collapse a complete evidence-bounded answer into a generic failure. Final prose remains Agent-owned and crosses only the existing security finalizer.
- Verification and rollout: Unit regressions cover the 2026-07-26 Beijing / 2026-07-25 New York weekend boundary, nearest Friday `2026-07-24`, unspecified-market non-assumption, and runtime prompt ordering. Agent prompt and finance CI regressions require the same target-session/latest-quote/scope separation in both natural and forced bounded finals. Exact commit `27ea2f53` was pushed, manifest-verified, and deployed, but its first fresh `美股为什么大跌` canary still persisted the generic failure after the provider emitted the exact canonical header and then requested additional read-only quote/Web evidence. D-2026-07-26-02 fixes that adjacent boundary. Production acceptance requires deploying the replacement exact revision, then replaying fresh broad-market, explicit-Friday, single-stock, and insufficient-cause samples with one answer/terminal, no generic failure, correct scope/date disclosure, byte-identical visible/history content, and zero active chats afterward.

## D-2026-07-26-02 Treat An Exact Finance Header As A Durable Boundary, Not A Stop Decision

- Status: Accepted; full repository gates passed, exact immutable replacement deployment and production acceptance remain required.
- Refines: `D-2026-07-21-01`, `D-2026-07-22-01`, `D-2026-07-26-01`, and `docs/adr/0004-agent-owned-research-loop.md`. The earlier rule that any tool delta after a model-authored canonical header is immediately terminal is superseded. The one-Agent loop, exact visible/persisted bytes, and fail-closed post-commit tool safety remain unchanged.
- Incident evidence: Fresh actor `codex-canary-27ea2f53-broad-1785052687` on exact deployed commit `27ea2f536ce16aab71c8cf2cfe48a92a13f89fb4` asked `美股为什么大跌`. Quote and Web calls succeeded, and the provider emitted the byte-exact service canonical line. In that same stream it then requested more quote evidence. The stream adapter immediately returned `agent_owned_finance_persistent_tool_error` before the completed tool batch reached the existing registered/structurally-valid/known-read-only validator, so Session persisted the canonical line plus `本轮研究未能完成，暂未形成可供参考的标的结论。`. This was not the older `committed terminal prefix mismatch`; it was a premature interpretation of a durable header as a `Stop` decision.
- Boundary rule: A successfully delivered exact canonical finance header is irreversible visible state, but only provider `Stop + Done` with a nonempty complete body is a natural final. If tool deltas follow the exact line in the same stream, keep the line visible, discard the abandoned body candidate, assemble the complete tool batch, and pass it through the same pre-frame validator used after a typed prefix. The batch may execute only if every tool is in the active registry, every argument payload parses and is structurally valid, and every call is known read-only. Unknown aliases, malformed calls, invalid DataFetch shapes, mixed batches, and persistent/write-capable tools still fail before assistant framing, observer notification, registry execution, or network access. An irreversible line is never reset or emitted twice.
- State promotion: After a safe continuation batch completes, an already delivered exact service prefix is promoted into the same request-local precommitted state without another downstream emit. Subsequent finance budget accounting and tool validation therefore cannot accidentally treat that visible line as speculative or reopen ordinary write-capable behavior.
- Verification and rollout: Agent regressions pair a safe same-stream Web continuation with an unsafe unknown-tool continuation. They prove the safe tool executes, the exact line survives without reset or duplicate commit, the same Agent reaches one natural final, and the unsafe tool executes zero times. Agent `125/125`, Channels `680/680`, Web `281/281`, Edge Worker typecheck plus `45/45`, finance CI `41/41`, workspace check/test, all CI-safe regressions, formatting, and diff checks pass; one unrelated Web-push read-through test failed once in the first workspace run, passed three isolated reruns, and the complete workspace rerun passed. Exact immutable replacement build, zero-active-chat restart, cloud/storage/auth checks, and the D-2026-07-26-01 broad/Friday/single-stock live matrix remain mandatory.

## D-2026-07-26-03 Hold Market-Move Bodies Behind A Mechanical Consistency Boundary

- Status: Accepted and production-verified on exact commit `84ca1f2114c059a157cd893c84067638c7618e84`.
- Refines: `D-2026-07-19-08`, `D-2026-07-21-01`, `D-2026-07-26-01`, `D-2026-07-26-02`, and `docs/adr/0004-agent-owned-research-loop.md`. It supersedes only the assumption that every complete Interactive market-move DirectFinal must publish without any pre-publication check. The one Agent/context, real current-turn tools, bounded research, absence of a handoff or separate terminal synthesizer, and exact visible/persisted-byte rules remain unchanged.
- Incident evidence: Exact replacement `cd78375bd6fbb70f3c0be87626cf291a470893a2` was pushed, manifest-verified, and deployed healthy. Fresh actor `codex-canary-cd78375b-broad-1785054103` asked `美股为什么大跌` and completed with one successful terminal, proving D-2026-07-26-02 removed the generic failure. The natural body was still unsafe to publish as a correct answer: it labeled `2026-07-24` as Thursday although that civil date was Friday, described title/summary-only secondary material as “已核验原因”, and carried a prior-week AI/semiconductor narrative into the target session. Prompt-only evidence ordering therefore did not enforce even mechanically decidable calendar and source-locality constraints.
- Visibility boundary: During an eligible finance DirectFinal stream, only bytes that complete the exact canonical header may reach the observer. The model-authored body remains request-local until `Stop + Done` and the checks below finish. A wrong partial header can still reset before commitment; a fully committed exact header remains irreversible and is never reset or duplicated. Ordinary non-market-move finance bodies are accepted at completion as before, while market-move bodies cross this narrower boundary.
- Mechanical checks: The checker activates only when the runtime contains the server-generated market-move calendar anchor. It validates every adjacent absolute-date/Chinese-weekday pair; resolves the target in priority order from an explicit absolute date, an explicit-weekday civil candidate, the modal/latest current-turn quote `market_date_new_york`, and only then the draft; requires a broad US-market answer to cover at least two representative broad/style index groups instead of substituting one sector or stock; checks quote-field claims; and inspects paragraphs that use deterministic causal language. Percentage claims must match `changesPercentage` rather than the absolute `change`; exchange claims must match structured exchange evidence; and an ordinary quote/provider timestamp cannot be described as a close, closing price, or closing session. Each definitive-cause paragraph must contain the target absolute date and an original URL actually present in a current-turn fetched-page result, and that result record must itself carry that date. A snippet-only result marked `full_page_content=false` or `kind=search_snippets` is ineligible. Source-date recognition includes ordinary numeric/Chinese forms and English forms such as `July 24, 2026`. Scenario, inference, candidate-cause, and explicit evidence-gap paragraphs are not treated as confirmed causes. These checks do not decide which event caused a move, grade investment prose, infer a trading calendar, or authorize facts from a URL alone.
- Correction and fallback: One invalid body may receive one hidden, bounded correction inside the same `Agent::run`, using the same context and existing evidence. The correction is constrained to a short answer and an enumerated set of current-result URLs that mechanically carry the target date. If a definitive cause failed locality and no such URL exists, the correction cannot improve the evidence and is skipped. The durable header remains precommitted. A final failure returns a deterministic narrow answer reconstructed only from current-turn structured quote fields: it preserves the exact header, the quote-anchored date, provider time, per-symbol changes and broad-versus-style scope mismatch, then says `原因本轮未完全核验`. It never uses the rejected draft's date/cause paragraphs or the generic research-failure suffix. This is the sole Interactive server-authored market-move fallback and must not expand into a general semantic reviewer, entity parser, format gate, or second terminal role.
- Verification and rollout: Intermediate exact deployments `f0281adb`, `e46a6bf4`, `12f1a924`, and `4139e12c` successively exposed quote-date fallback loss, exact-symbol/broad-floor deadlock, `change` versus `changesPercentage` confusion, unsupported close/exchange claims, snippet promotion, and a duplicate-search latency overrun. Final commit `84ca1f2114c059a157cd893c84067638c7618e84` passed the complete workspace check/test contract, Web `286/286`, Edge Worker `45/45`, finance contracts `42/42`, CI-safe regressions, formatting, and diff checks; its immutable 504-file runtime payload was hash-verified and deployed with authoritative cloud storage/auth health. Four fresh actors for unverified-rumor Friday, broad `美股为什么大跌`, explicit-Friday broad decline, and `HIMS` Friday decline all finished in `45.597–58.917s` with exactly one successful terminal, no reset/error/partial/generic failure, byte-identical two-row history, and zero active chats. They preserved target `2026-07-24`, corrected the false broad-crash premise from structured quotes, and returned `原因本轮未完全核验` instead of promoting snippet-only causes.

## D-2026-07-26-04 Let Whop Own Discord Membership Fulfillment

- Status: Superseded by `D-2026-08-04-01`; retained as historical evidence only.
- Refined by: `D-2026-07-26-06` adds a separate Whop-to-HONE
  application-entitlement webhook and email activation flow. It does not make
  the HONE Discord bot a role owner or change Whop's native Discord lifecycle.
- Context: The canonical international membership is sold by Whop, and the
  target Discord server already has a dedicated paid-member role and a Whop
  integration. At the time of this decision the repository had no Whop
  application-entitlement ledger, while `hone-discord` was and remains a
  messaging/agent bot rather than a role-lifecycle service.
- Decision: Include Whop's native Discord app in product
  `prod_9jQsUKaifh6ZA`. Connect it to Discord guild
  `1391380994182877205`, include role `VIP 付费用户`, log to channel
  `1423211147674517537`, and use Whop's `Remove Role` cancellation action.
  Whop owns Discord account linking, claim access, join, grant, and removal.
  Do not add a custom billing webhook or make the HONE bot a second Discord
  role source merely to duplicate this lifecycle.
- Boundary: HONE application entitlements are not granted by this integration.
  If paid application features are required, build a separate verified
  entitlement ledger/webhook flow with its own idempotency, signature,
  reconciliation, refund/dispute, and audit contracts.
- Operations: Keep the Whop integration role above the managed VIP role, grant
  the VIP role no administrative permissions, restrict the Whop event-log
  channel, and troubleshoot from Whop membership state plus event logs before
  applying a manual Discord role. Archived Whop products do not inherit this
  access unless explicitly migrated.
- Verification: The authenticated product editor shows Discord included; the
  native app settings show the target server, included VIP role, event log, and
  cancellation removal; member preview shows the linked-account selector and
