- Isolation decision: Worktrees keep separate writable Cargo targets because revision provenance requires build inputs and outputs to stay isolated under concurrent work. A global shared target is rejected as a disk shortcut. A future content-addressed compiler cache such as `sccache` may be evaluated separately, but is not required by the local source workflow.
- Retention decision: Successful source deployment maintains `current` and `previous` symlinks, then best-effort prunes only older immediate directories named by an exact 40-character lowercase hexadecimal revision. A candidate is removable only when every direct child is one of the three expected binaries or `manifest.json`, each as a regular non-symlink file. Unknown names, extra files, nested paths, symlinks, and cleanup failures remain untouched. Pruning occurs after the runtime is verified, the new current link is committed, and rollback is disarmed; a failed deploy never changes retention state.
- Test boundary: The external deployment simulator controls launchd, process/PID, locks, HTTP readiness, target layout, and filesystem state. It proves the source-runtime artifact path and manifest, current/previous retention, safe old-release deletion, suspicious/unknown retention, and no pruning on rollback. Cargo's own verbose command output supplies the profile-flag evidence; measured disk totals are recorded as machine-local acceptance evidence rather than frozen into a brittle CI size threshold.

## D-2026-08-03-04 Guarantee One Substantive Research Round Before Any Bounded Finance Final

- Status: Accepted and locally verified.
- Problem: `820a7240` bounded Interactive finance to three tool rounds, 24 calls, 20 DataFetch calls, and six Web calls to cut time-to-first-token. Rounds were charged from the first DataFetch onward, so entity resolution and research competed for the same budget. When the first token of a question was not a security — `美股科技股和半导体股票方面的CTA是多少`, where `CTA` is a managed-futures term — the scanner seeded it as an explicit code, the entity-first contract required exact-symbol lookups, the registry had no coverage, and the three rounds were spent before a single `web_search` ran. The tools-disabled final then produced `无法提供具体的数字`: an answer indistinguishable from an offline model on a turn where every tool worked.
- Budget decision: Identity resolution is a precondition of research, not research. Identity-only rounds draw on their own two-round budget; the three-round research budget covers only substantive work. Inside the bounded loop the last two global call slots are reserved for `web_search`, and a reserve or per-tool rejection is not exhaustion while open research still has budget. Total work stays hard-bounded by 2 + 3 rounds, the unchanged call ceilings, and `max_iterations`.
- Invariant decision: A finance turn must never reach a tools-disabled final having executed only identity searches. When the budget is reached with zero substantive business calls, the same Agent gets one bounded open-research round with `data_fetch` removed from the tool list and an instruction to research the user's actual question. Blocked-batch safety finalization keeps its own path and is not delayed by this rescue.
- Seed-confidence decision: Clause-subject grammar alone proves only that a token sits in a sentence that mentions securities somewhere. Such a seed is emitted as tentative, never as an explicit code, and a turn whose seeds are all tentative carries an explicit low-confidence clause telling the Agent these may be macro/strategy/indicator/industry acronyms and must be abandoned rather than resolved at the cost of the turn. A maintained non-security acronym deny-list is rejected as the fix: it cannot enumerate the class, and every miss reproduces this failure.
- Listing: the mechanical current-listing publication boundary is owned by `D-2026-08-03-02`; this decision adds nothing to it. The two are complementary — that one stops a denial the evidence contradicts, this one stops a turn from reaching the final with no evidence at all.
- Prompt-budget decision: `67b9a915` compacted `soul.md` and deleted `每个公司或证券问题先调用 DataFetch \`search\``, which acceptance check `20.current-data-capability` guards; the compaction reached `main` with that check red. The rule is restored, duplicated `entity_route` protocol text that also lives in the Agent instructions is compacted instead, and the file is kept under the reviewed budget with headroom. The reviewed 12,000-character budget with near-zero headroom is itself a hazard: the next compaction is again likelier to delete a guarded rule than to delete duplication.
- Verification: `unresolvable_identity_never_closes_a_finance_turn_without_open_research`, `finance_loop_keeps_identity_rounds_off_the_research_budget_before_forcing_final`, `clause_subject_grammar_alone_yields_a_tentative_seed_not_an_explicit_code`, `a_bound_uppercase_ticker_stays_an_explicit_code`, and `all_tentative_seeds_add_a_low_confidence_discovery_clause`. The first fails against the pre-fix constants. Full `hone-agent` (142) and `hone-channels` (727) library suites, the repository-wide `--lib` suite, all 44 finance acceptance contracts (previously 43 with `20` red), and `tests/regression/run_ci.sh` pass.

## D-2026-08-03-05 Make Every Scheduled-Push Store Visible At The Cancel Boundary

- Status: Accepted and locally verified.
- Problem: Feishu users repeatedly asked to turn scheduled tasks off, were told they were off, and kept receiving pushes. Nothing failed to persist. `cron_job` owns cron/heartbeat jobs; event pushes and the 08:30/09:00 unified digest slots live in notification prefs and survive `remove_all`. `list` returned `jobs: []`, `remove_all` returned `remaining_count: 0`, and `response_finalizer` rendered those into the user-visible sentences `你当前没有定时任务。` and `已删除全部 N 个定时或心跳任务。` — true about the cron store, false as the user reads them. An actor who never set `digest_slots` is on the system default schedule, so the surviving pushes were ones they never knowingly enabled.
- Decision: `cron_job` `list`/`remove_all` carry an `automatic_push` summary built from the notification-prefs store: `all_automatic_push_stopped`, named `remaining_sources`, `event_push_enabled`, `digest_source` (`user` / `system_default` / `disabled`), `digest_times`, and `stop_all_action`. `remaining_count: 0` keeps its narrow cron meaning and stops being evidence of anything else. Service-authored confirmations append the surviving sources whenever `all_automatic_push_stopped` is false and stay unqualified when it is true. The cron prompt policy forbids "已全部关闭"-class wording unless that flag is true, and routes a repeated "关不掉" straight to `notification_prefs(action="disable_all")` instead of another `remove_all` with the same confirmation.
- Relation to `D-2026-08-03-04`: same defect class. A component answered a narrower question than the user asked while the surrounding text presented it as a complete answer. Both fixes attach the missing scope to the tool result rather than relying on the model to remember the gap.
- Rejected: silently having `cron_job(remove_all)` also disable event pushes and digests. Deleting scheduled tasks is not consent to turn off every notification channel; the user must see what remains and say so.
- Verification: `cron_removal_discloses_digest_and_event_pushes_that_still_fire`, `cron_removal_reports_a_full_stop_once_every_push_source_is_off`, `remove_all_confirmation_discloses_push_sources_that_survive_it`, `empty_cron_list_does_not_read_as_no_automatic_pushes`, `a_full_stop_confirmation_stays_unqualified`. Full `hone-tools` (168) and `hone-channels` (730) suites, the repository-wide `--lib` suite, and all 44 finance acceptance contracts pass.

## D-2026-08-03-06 Require Current-Turn Evidence Before Any Self-Declared Research Answer

- Status: Accepted and locally verified. Supersedes the phrase-matched draft of this decision.
- Problem: A probe of `coreweave 现在怎么样` found that a turn can answer from model memory with `iterations=1, tool_calls_made=0, tool_choice_modes=[Auto]` and publish as success. The first attempt at a fix detected the bad answer by vocabulary — an eighteen-entry list of `未上市` / IPO / private-company phrasings plus a corporate-suffix stopword list. That was the same anti-pattern this log had already rejected for non-security acronyms in `D-2026-08-03-04`, and a second probe proved why: `CRWV 当前股价 142.30 美元，Forward PE 约 38 倍，目标价 175 美元` with zero tool calls published untouched, because no denial word appeared in it. A vocabulary can only ever cover the claim types that have already been reported.
- Decision: Gate on the Agent's own declaration, not on what the claim says. The answer contract already requires an investment answer to open with the exact `数据时间：北京时间 …；行情口径：` line and tells the Agent to skip that format entirely when the turn is not an investment request. A final carrying that header in a turn that executed no business tool has answered a research question from memory; it is withheld for one bounded retry whose tool choice is `Required`. One precondition, no vocabulary, no classifier.
- Coverage: the same single rule now catches the listing denial, the IPO/private-company phrasing, fabricated price/multiple/target-price answers, and the `无法提供具体的数字` refusal that opened this investigation. Ordinary chat never carries the header and is untouched — measured as `tools=0, iterations=1` with no forced round. A blocked-batch turn keeps its own safety finalization and is excluded, since it attempted tools and must not be pushed into another round.
- Removed: the eighteen-entry denial phrase list and the corporate-suffix stopword list added by the earlier draft. `listing_denial_line` is restored to its upstream form, where it serves only the remaining legitimate case — deciding whether an answer contradicts listing evidence the turn actually holds. Company-name matching is kept but is derived from the provider payload (`company_name`, and the segment before its first comma), not from a maintained word list.
- Rejected: forcing tools on round one for every turn. That would either need a request-side classifier — the exact mechanism behind the CTA false positive — or would make a greeting call a tool. Keying on the answer's own frame needs neither.
- Verification: `research_evidence_precondition_ignores_what_the_claim_says` (five unrelated claim types caught by one rule, two chat answers untouched, and the rule standing down once any tool ran), `memory_only_listing_claim_is_sent_back_with_tools_required` (retry is `Required`, published answer no longer denies the listing), `listing_denial_by_company_name_is_caught_like_a_symbol_denial`, and `company_name_labels_come_from_the_provider_payload_only`. Full `hone-agent` (147) suite, the repository-wide `--lib` suite, and all 44 finance acceptance contracts pass.

## D-2026-08-03-07 Let The Registry Decide Which Tokens Are Securities

- Status: Accepted and locally verified.
- Problem: after `D-2026-08-03-06` removed the denial-phrase vocabulary, three more maintained token lists remained in the Interactive seeding path — `is_non_security_acronym`, `identifier_requires_explicit_security_binding`, `is_identifier_grammar_word` — plus a security-context marker list gating two positional bindings. They are the same anti-pattern, and `CTA` reaching the Agent as a settled code is what opened this whole investigation.
- Measurement: over twenty representative queries, deleting all three acronym lists changed exactly one row, and that row became a low-confidence candidate rather than a settled code. Deleting the context marker list additionally *restored* recall the list had been destroying: `how about AAPL`, `what do you think about NVDA` and `NVDA 的 PE 是多少` had produced no seed at all. The lists were not protecting the pipeline; they were mistuned to a handful of test cases while dropping real tickers.
- Decision: code shape is judged structurally — upper case, adjacency to non-ASCII text, or being the entire query. Which code is actually a security is decided by the Agent's registry lookup, not by a vocabulary. Acronyms and ordinary words alike arrive as candidates; the Interactive guarantee is that the scope never closes into a deterministic `Securities` decision, and the Agent dismisses the rest with a real search. This is affordable only because identity-only rounds stopped consuming the research budget in `D-2026-08-03-04` and evidence became a publication precondition in `D-2026-08-03-06`; a false candidate now costs one free identity round instead of a wrong answer.
- Accepted cost: `stock`, `hi` and `about` can appear as low-confidence candidates. That is deliberate — `nbis`, `isrg` and `googl` are structurally identical to them, and choosing to drop the words would drop the tickers. Tests that asserted exact seed vectors now assert that the intended ticker is present, since co-occurring candidates are expected.
- Retained: one consolidated deterministic brake, applied only when `origin != Interactive`. Typed scheduled and heartbeat work turns mentions into a contract with no model in the loop, so an unchecked `PCE` or `GDP` would silently become a security; the macro-digest regression proves that path still needs it. It merges the three deleted lists into a single function and is the only maintained token set left in the file. Removing it requires routing scheduled entity resolution through the same Agent loop, which is a separate change to the typed-contract pipeline.
- Verification: 242 deleted list entries, 350 lines removed against 238 added. Interactive seeds re-measured across sixteen queries; scheduled macro-digest and event-condition seeds unchanged. Full `hone-channels` (729 of 730, one pre-existing unrelated race) and repository-wide `--lib` suites plus all 44 finance acceptance contracts pass.

## D-2026-08-03-08 Enrich The Turn Before The Agent Thinks, Not After It Answers

- Status: Accepted and locally verified.
- Problem: `D-2026-08-03-06` stopped an evidence-free research answer by detecting it and forcing a retry. That works, but it spends an extra model round and still depends on the model complying on the retry. The Interactive branch of `prepare_verified_investment_turn` executed no tool at all — it appended prompt text and returned — so the Agent's first thinking round was always evidence-free by construction, and every guard in the loop was a reaction to that.
- Decision: run one unconditional evidence pass before the first model call. An open `web_search` on the user's own words runs in parallel with `data_fetch(search)` for each scanner candidate; a candidate the registry resolves to exactly one symbol earns a `snapshot`. Results are injected as a labelled context block. Detection becomes the safety net rather than the mechanism.
- Non-binding by construction: the stage returns no contract, fixes no entity and constrains no answer. The injected block states that the results may be irrelevant, that unresolved candidates should be abandoned rather than pursued, and that remaining evidence is the Agent's to gather. This is the distinction from the Interactive preflight removed by `D-2026-07-19-08`: that one decided entities server-side and bound the answer to a typed contract; this one only adds context. `D-2026-07-19-08` removed a service-imposed *completion* pipeline and explicitly preserved current-turn evidence ownership, so pre-loading does not reopen it.
- Registry decides, not the service: a candidate becomes a market-data lookup only when the registry returns exactly one symbol for it. Multiple or zero hits contribute the raw search result and nothing more, which keeps `D-2026-08-03-07` intact — the service still never rules on what is a security.
- Accounting: preloaded evidence lives in the turn input, not in `tool_calls_made`, so `AgentRunnerRequest` carries `preloaded_evidence_calls` through to the loop. Without it the `D-2026-08-03-06` precondition would reject a turn that answered from a rich preloaded context. Payloads carrying a provider error are not counted as evidence.
- Bounds: at most three candidates, a wall-clock deadline for the whole stage, and bounded per-item serialization. Exceeding the deadline or failing a provider logs internally and continues as an ordinary Agent-owned turn; the user never waits on it and never sees it fail.
- Verification: `pre_turn_enrichment_loads_evidence_as_context_not_as_a_contract` proves the candidate still arrives as a candidate, that no contract is produced, that a credential-less environment preloads nothing and still succeeds, and that no auxiliary model is involved. The shared FMP stub now serves unenumerated requests an empty payload, since the service legitimately issues lookups a test did not list. Full `hone-channels` (730) and repository-wide `--lib` suites, all 44 finance acceptance contracts, and `tests/regression/run_ci.sh` pass.

## D-2026-08-03-09 Make Search Recency A Provider Constraint And Carry The Market's Local Date

- Status: Accepted and locally verified.
- Problem: `D-2026-08-03-08` made every Interactive turn run a pre-turn `web_search`, but the only recency signal was a date pasted into the query text and `WebSearchTool` sent Tavily no time parameter at all — a stale page could still rank first. The query also carried the Beijing timestamp including a time of day, giving the engine a literal `09:31` to match on, and used the Beijing date alone. For a US-market product that is wrong twice over: a Beijing morning is still the previous New York session, so the anchor names a US date that has not happened yet.
- Tool decision: `web_search` accepts an optional `time_range` (`day`/`week`/`month`/`year`) and passes it through to the provider. It stays optional and unset by default, so a definitional, historical or long-horizon fundamental question is not stripped of authoritative older sources; the schema tells the Agent to set it for time-sensitive work and to omit it otherwise. This is the Agent's judgment to exercise, not a server-side rule.
- Enrichment decision: the pre-turn pass sets `time_range=week` explicitly, and builds its query from absolute dates only — the Beijing date plus the New York local date when the two differ, never a time of day. The builder is a separate function so the date behaviour is unit-tested rather than inferred from an integration path that cannot run without provider credentials.
- Verification: `pre_turn_web_query_carries_both_market_dates` (Beijing anchor present, time of day absent, New York date present exactly when it differs) and `time_range_is_an_opt_in_provider_constraint` (parameter exists, is not required, and offers only the four provider windows). Full `hone-tools` and `hone-channels` suites, the repository-wide `--lib` suite, all 44 finance acceptance contracts, and `tests/regression/run_ci.sh` pass.

## D-2026-08-03-10 Name The Work In Progress On Public Chat

- Status: Accepted and locally verified.
- Problem: `D-2026-08-03-08` added a pre-turn evidence pass, which buys correctness with latency. Feishu already narrates the tool it is running; public chat collapsed every tool into one of two fixed strings, so a user watching a multi-second research turn saw `正在查询并核验所需数据` regardless of whether it was searching the web, confirming an entity or reading a quote. Worse, the new enrichment pass runs before any runner exists and therefore emits no runner tool status at all, leaving the whole enrichment budget as a silent window.
- Decision: name the work by tool kind in product language — search, entity confirmation, market data, news, financials, fund holdings, sector data, holdings, reminders, analysis methods, local research notes — and emit dedicated session stages around the pre-turn pass so that window is narrated too. The runner label is read only to select a phrase; no internal tool name, raw argument, symbol or provider detail reaches the browser.
- Placement: the browser already renders this beside the pending turn and switches to the answer once content streams, so nothing here enters the message body and the typewriter behaviour is unchanged. No frontend change was needed.
- Verification: `public_tool_status_names_the_work_without_leaking_internals` covers ten tool kinds plus the unknown-tool fallback and asserts no internal token survives into the user-visible string; `pre_turn_enrichment_window_is_not_silent` pins the enrichment stages; the existing public-safety test now asserts the named wording still carries no provider detail or reasoning. Full `hone-web-api` (169) and repository-wide `--lib` suites plus all 44 finance acceptance contracts pass.

## D-2026-08-03-11 Make The Persisted Codex Native ID The Sole Conversation Identity

- Status: Accepted and locally verified.
- Supersedes: the native generation boundary and `session/new -> session/resume -> session/new` verification expectation in `D-2026-08-01-01`, plus older pre-marker rotation language in `D-2026-07-30-01`. It does not change the current-turn-only user payload, Codex developer-instruction layer, versioned stream dialects, or OpenCode's separate fresh-session model.
- Identity decision: one deterministic persistent Hone `SessionIdentity` owns one Codex native ID. Any nonempty persisted `codex_acp_session_id` is authoritative regardless of `codex_acp_session_mode`, instruction fingerprint, code revision, process restart, or native compaction. Mode and fingerprint remain bounded audit/migration facts and are updated in place. Only missing ID permits `session/new`; resume failure remains fail closed and never falls back to new.
- Durability decision: runner storage access remains forbidden. `ExecutionService` gives persistent requests a narrow host-owned `AgentSessionMetadataCheckpoint`; transient requests receive none. After `session/new` returns, Codex checkpoints ID, current mode, fingerprint, and already-observed bounded adapter metadata to authoritative Hone session storage before sending the first `session/prompt`. Checkpoint failure stops before prompt. A restart after prompt dispatch therefore reloads and resumes the same ID.
- Retry decision: a native persistent transport error or empty result does not prove the thread rejected the current prompt. `AgentSession` never automatically resends that turn; ordinary replay runners retain their bounded safe retry behavior. This closes the same-process stale-request path that could otherwise call `session/new` twice after a successful checkpoint.
- Probe decision: real Codex acceptance probes must run with an isolated temporary `CODEX_HOME` and reuse only the existing authentication file. Their sessions, logs, cache, and state are deleted with the temp directory and never enter the user's primary Codex Desktop task index. Production continues to use its configured persistent Codex home.
- Version boundary: the external contract remains explicitly Codex CLI `0.146.0` plus `@agentclientprotocol/codex-acp 1.1.7`. The executable stdio double proves `new -> resume -> resume` across compact and changed developer instructions, enforces checkpoint-before-prompt, simulates a post-prompt process crash and restart, and proves checkpoint/resume failures cannot fork. A changed adapter version requires a new named capture instead of silently freezing current Rust implementation details.
- Verification: focused identity/checkpoint/no-auto-retry tests, `hone-channels` (`738 passed`, one host-dependent ignore), workspace all-target check/test, Web (`347`), Edge typecheck/tests (`45`), all `44` finance acceptance contracts, the complete CI-safe regression suite, shell syntax, changed-file format, and diff checks passed. Real isolated Codex CLI and codex-acp probes completed against exact installed versions `0.146.0` / `1.1.7` without writing their acceptance sessions into the primary task index.

## D-2026-08-03-12 Speculate The First Candidate's Market Data In The Same Batch

- Status: Accepted and locally verified.
- Problem: the pre-turn evidence pass costs two sequential provider round-trips — identity search first, market data second — and the second one almost always asks for the symbol the user already typed. Overlapping the pass with turn preparation was investigated and rejected: `restore_runtime_context`, `resolve_prompt_input_at` and `ExecutionService::prepare` are all synchronous, so there is nothing meaningful to await alongside. The serial cost is inside the pass, not around it.
- Decision: fire a speculative `snapshot` for the first candidate in the same batch as the identity searches and the web search. When the registry resolves that candidate to itself the speculative result is reused and the second round-trip disappears; otherwise it is discarded and the normal second round runs. Speculation is limited to the first candidate so a miss wastes at most one provider call.
- Correctness boundary: the speculative result is reused only when the registry resolved that exact candidate to a provider-equivalent symbol. A candidate that resolves elsewhere must never be answered with market data fetched for the user's raw token — that would attach another company's quote to the answer.
- Test-stub consequence: the shared FMP stub stopped accepting once every configured route had been served once, which held while the Agent was the only caller. The service now calls first, so those requests retired the routes and left the Agent talking to a closed listener. The stub now runs until the test releases it, and keeps its separate assertion that every configured route was exercised.
- Verification: `speculative_snapshot_is_discarded_when_the_registry_resolves_elsewhere` configures a decoy quote for the raw token and asserts the answer context carries the resolved symbol's snapshot and never the decoy price; it fails when the speculation guard is disabled. Full `hone-channels` (734) and repository-wide `--lib` suites, all 44 finance acceptance contracts, and `tests/regression/run_ci.sh` pass.
- Proxy follow-up: loopback FMP adapters and test stubs bypass workstation HTTP proxies while external provider URLs retain normal proxy behavior. This prevents a local proxy from swallowing the positive-path requests and turning a deterministic regression into a 12-second evidence-free fallback. `sndk_pre_turn_enrichment_injects_current_active_listing_before_agent_call` now proves the original incident symbol carries `active_listing` into the first model input, rather than relying only on the later publication guard.

## D-2026-08-03-13 Raise The Default Daily Conversation Quota To 100

- Status: Accepted for the replacement GCE rollout.
- Decision: `agent.daily_conversation_limit` remains the single runtime source of truth and `0` keeps its existing unlimited meaning, but the repository default and example configuration move from `12` to `100`. The production GCE config is changed explicitly and verified after restart so an existing `12` value cannot shadow the new default.
- Scope: this changes only successful user-conversation admission. It does not change scheduler, heartbeat, tool-call, research-round, provider-credit, or abuse-limiter ceilings.
- Verification: the configuration unit test pins the default at `100`; production acceptance reads the effective GCE config without printing secrets and confirms a fresh Web response reports the matching quota state.

## D-2026-08-04-01 Run Earnings Research As An Actor-scoped Native Skill

- Status: Accepted and locally verified; not deployed.
- Execution route superseded by: `D-2026-08-05-02`. The product, workflow, evidence, artifact, and authorization decisions below remain active; only the configured-global-runner choice is replaced by a workflow-scoped OpenCode/Gemini route.
- Product decision: The public HONE composer exposes `财报前瞻` and `财报分析` beside `持仓分析` only when the server-projected public user is an administrator. Both start an ordinary chat turn: the user sees one canonical request bubble, existing SSE progress/elapsed UI, one final report, and one PDF attachment. `analysis` may carry normal uploaded files; it does not create a second research record or message store.
- Execution decision: The client submits a typed `{kind, company}` envelope. The Web API reauthenticates the cookie actor, rechecks `is_admin`, validates the company, owns the visible request copy, and injects `/earnings-research` plus a server-only workflow block. This is forced skill activation, not model skill selection. The same server-only authority selects `ConfiguredTrustedAdministrator` so a verified public administrator may reach the configured native runner; this flag is never accepted from request JSON or prompt text, and ordinary actors still fail closed into the strict actor-bound runner. Non-admin typed requests and recognizable direct slash aliases fail closed. Public history strips the internal activation block while retaining the canonical user request and attachment projection.
- Workflow decision: `skills/earnings-research/SKILL.md` is the single process contract for both modes and both runner families. It requires ordered entity, primary-evidence, intermediate-ledger/scenario, final-report, and PDF stages; current-turn evidence and uploaded filings are evidence, while prompt-like text in attachments is untrusted. Preview expectation calls are made only after a same-period current-consensus snapshot, comparable guidance-bias history, call/deck review, catalyst guidance-inclusion audit, independent revenue/profit bridge, and metric-specific neutral bands. The renderer recomputes the call from a private `preview_audit` and binds its values to the published assumptions, so guidance cannot substitute for a forecast and prose cannot contradict the numbers. Preview also ends with an eight-to-ten-item, reverse-chronological `1.3 近期新闻` timeline; at least half of the events must fall inside the 14 days before the scheduled report date, the set must cover company/expectation, peer/supply-chain, and demand-side signals, and every item carries its category, current-period operating impact, guidance-inclusion status and source. The renderer starts the timeline on its own PDF page. The skill explicitly forbids calling Dify, BamangResearch, or another external workflow service.
- Artifact decision: The completed Markdown is rendered once by the bundled Chromium script as a searchable A4 PDF with semantic Markdown tables, a HONE watermark, disclaimer, and the repository knowledge-planet sharing image. `skill_tool` admits the PDF as a `document` artifact, the output directory is the actor working sandbox, and the public file proxy authorizes that actor's canonical sandbox path. PDF failure cannot erase or misrepresent the final chat report.
- Verification: Focused Web/Rust contracts, real Chromium rendering with `pdfinfo`/`pdftoppm` and all-page visual inspection, and local browser acceptance cover admin/non-admin visibility, both dialogs, file selection, typed request bodies, loading, final report, and PDF card. A real isolated SNDK administrator run on the validated Codex CLI `0.146.0` / codex-acp `1.1.7` boundary selected `codex_acp`, completed 23 tool calls and the native skill, and produced the inspected five-page PDF. This acceptance exposed and then closed both the server-verified-admin runner-selection gap and the PDF Markdown-table rendering gap. No production deployment was performed.

## D-2026-08-04-02 Keep Earnings PDFs Workflow-dense And Chat Downloads Direct

- Status: Accepted and locally verified; not deployed.
- Visual decision: Earnings PDFs reuse the former Workflow report's visual grammar without rasterizing the document: dense searchable Chinese body copy, warm-beige header/footer bands, peach section bars, restrained dark-navy typography, repeated section/page markers, a diagonal `知识星球：巴芒科技` watermark, and a dedicated final knowledge-planet sharing page. HONE remains the report brand; the change does not relax the native skill's evidence, expectation-audit, news-freshness, or report-structure contracts.
- Download decision: Non-image assistant attachments remain same-origin links to the existing actor-authorized public file proxy. The PDF card carries the download filename and no longer opens a second browser tab. The browser contract separately verifies the authenticated URL returns `application/pdf` with a `%PDF-` header and that clicking the card emits the expected Chinese filename.
- Execution boundary: Native Codex may discover and apply the projected skill itself, but repository script execution stays on the trusted Hone MCP host through `skill_tool`. The MCP child receives an absolute `HONE_SKILLS_DIR` derived from the canonical config location, because its working directory is the actor sandbox. Earnings instructions forbid direct sandbox Chrome, dependency installation, and alternate ReportLab/Swift/browser renderers; a renderer failure remains truthful instead of silently changing the artifact implementation.
- Verification boundary: A current-source isolated backend completed a real administrator SNDK preview from the public dialog on Codex CLI `0.146.0` / codex-acp `1.1.7`. The host skill script produced a 620338-byte, five-page A4 PDF; all pages were rendered and inspected, all expose searchable text and the exact `知识星球：巴芒科技` watermark, the news occupies pages 3–4, and the original sharing image occupies page 5. The same assistant reply showed a direct PDF card and the browser click path was exercised. The focused PDF regressions, Web tests, Rust tests, and production build pass. The isolated service was stopped and no deployment was performed.

## D-2026-08-05-01 Replace A Codex Binding Only When The Adapter Proves Its Rollout Is Absent

- Status: Accepted, deployed, and production verified.
- Extends: `D-2026-08-03-11` and ADR 0002. The normal one-SessionIdentity/one-native-ID rule, current-turn-only prompt ownership, pre-prompt metadata checkpoint and no automatic prompt resend remain unchanged.
- Incident: production HONE retained 154 distinct Codex native bindings after the service user's Codex rollout state had been cleared during authentication recovery; only one bound ID still existed locally. A Caris Life Sciences earnings-analysis turn correctly reached Codex ACP `1.1.7`, but `session/resume` failed before Skill execution with structured details `no rollout found for thread id <persisted id>`. Generic user output hid that diagnosis behind the normal failure message.
- Decision: preserve bounded, redacted ACP `error.data.details` in the internal protocol error. If and only if the validated adapter rejects `session/resume` before prompt with exactly `no rollout found for thread id <the same persisted id>`, create one replacement native session in that same adapter process and checkpoint the replacement ID, mode, fingerprint and observed profile before sending the current prompt. The old binding is unusable, so replacing it restores the one-usable-ID invariant rather than treating configuration drift or an ambiguous transport failure as authority to fork.
- Negative boundary: generic `Internal error`, a detail naming another ID, matching stderr text, timeout, authentication/permission failure, process exit, empty result and any post-prompt failure remain fail-closed. Hone never resends a native prompt automatically. A checkpoint failure still stops before prompt.
- Operational repair: an operator may clear only the three bounded ACP binding fields after an owner-only backup and an explicit absent-rollout comparison; HONE chat history and messages remain authoritative and are never deleted. The Caris binding was backed up and cleared independently so the affected user can recover before the new runtime is deployed.
- Verification: the executable Codex ACP `1.1.7` fixture proves missing-rollout `resume -> new -> checkpoint -> prompt`, exact-ID matching, structured-detail redaction and ordinary resume failure without `session/new`. The complete repository gates and main CI passed. Production runs exact revision `f819584c`; a real Caris Life Sciences analysis completed Skill/data/Web execution, persisted one PDF attachment, survived a same-revision service restart, and downloaded again from restored chat history. The completed plan is archived at `docs/archive/plans/codex-acp-missing-rollout-recovery.md`.

## D-2026-08-05-02 Route Earnings Workflows To Gemini 3.1 Pro Through OpenCode

- Status: Deployed and production verified.
- Supersedes: only the global configured-runner execution choice in `D-2026-08-04-01`. The administrator gate, forced `earnings-research` skill, evidence workflow, PDF contract, actor ownership, and no-external-workflow boundary remain unchanged.
- Problem: model override alone cannot move an earnings turn from a globally configured Codex ACP runner to Gemini. Passing `google/gemini-3.1-pro-preview` to Codex would select an invalid Codex model, while changing the global runner would also change ordinary chat. A per-turn runner switch also changes context ownership: Codex needs one current native turn, whereas OpenCode needs Hone's fresh ephemeral compiled replay containing the invoked skill and retained context.
- Decision: `agent.earnings_workflow` owns the exact trusted route and defaults to `runner=opencode_acp`, `model=google/gemini-3.1-pro-preview`. Only the Web API branch that has reauthenticated a public administrator and recognized the structured/direct earnings activation may create the internal `AgentRunRunnerOverride`. `AgentSession` rejects that override without verified administrator prompt authority, disables native-Codex input/observation behavior for the turn, and `ExecutionService` constructs OpenCode independently of the global runner. Ordinary chat continues using its existing global runner and model.
- Credential boundary: the model string is safe configuration; the OpenRouter API key is not. Runtime reuses the config-owned `llm.providers.openrouter.api_key/api_keys` pool, injects the selected key into the OpenCode child only, and derives the transport model `openrouter/google/gemini-3.1-pro-preview`. No provider key may be committed, passed as a command argument, written into protocol fixtures, or logged. A missing credential or invalid workflow route fails truthfully instead of falling back to another provider/model.
- Skill/PDF consequence: the earnings skill is runner-neutral. It assembles the complete render JSON in memory and calls the host-side `skill_tool` script boundary on Codex or OpenCode; it does not require an actor-local spec file and does not launch a fallback renderer.
- Structured-payload consequence: the earnings runner passes the complete report object as `script_payload`; the trusted host serializes it once into the renderer's single JSON argument. The model must not stringify or manually escape that payload. This keeps large preview audits runner-neutral and avoids transport failures caused by malformed model-authored JSON strings.
- Context-isolation consequence: the typed earnings route remains in the user's existing chat for UI and durable history, but its dedicated runner receives no prior durable messages or compact summary. A completed historical instruction is not background evidence for a new, self-contained company/mode workflow and must not be left for Gemini to distinguish from current work. Ordinary chat and non-earnings direct skill use keep their existing history behavior.
- System-contract consequence: the trusted earnings route also bypasses generic Interactive investment preloading and appends a same-level system override that makes the skill's old Workflow headings, no-timestamp opening, evidence families, and successful official PDF the only terminal contract. This is required because a lower-level skill instruction cannot safely override the repository-wide timestamp-first system rule. A correctable renderer rejection must be repaired inside the same model/tool loop; a text-only fallback is not a completed earnings workflow.
- Original-workflow consequence: the old Dify `V2-财报前瞻` prompt is the primary content decision path, not a loose style reference. A preview first calls beat/miss/in-line from the audited independent forecast, then explains the result through historical guidance delivery, current guidance, management commentary and company-specific order/capacity evidence. `1.2.3` must compare actual issuing institutions, dates, ratings, targets and same-quarter forecast disclosure with management guidance and the independent forecast; a consensus number, publisher, columnist or conference appearance cannot substitute for an institution view.
- Content-parity consequence: the live `V2-财报前瞻` main prompt and its linked `公司近期新闻时间线分析模块` are the governing content specifications. `1.2.1` must begin with the same explicit expectation call as the opening; `1.2.2` must publish decision-useful revenue growth plus profit/EPS or margin assumptions; and every institution in `1.2.3` must have its own absolute date, rating, target/disclosure and conservative/aggressive comparison. A forecast-consensus distance equal to the neutral band remains `与分析师持平` under a scale-aware epsilon. Forecast-bridge deltas require company/regulatory-primary evidence or explicit model arithmetic plus a recognition basis, so cumulative order/backlog/capacity values cannot silently become current-quarter revenue.
- News consequence: the dedicated news section uses eight to ten reverse-chronological natural paragraphs with plain source names and no published URLs. At least six and at least 60% are company-direct, including the prior earnings/call and a named issuing-institution view; at least five use company/regulatory primary evidence or the named issuing institution, and commentary is capped at two. Each paragraph has at least three substantive sentences and preserves the linked Workflow's event interpretation, current-quarter/short-term effect, longer-term effect, product/competitive impact and next verification signal. Customer, peer and supply-chain items are capped at three and require an evidenced company-specific transmission into the reporting period; publisher-author ratings, price-only moves, conference attendance, generic sector sentiment and unrelated customer spending are rejected in both the private audit and final prose. News depth may add pages; it must not be compressed away to preserve a one-page layout.
- Terminal-enforcement consequence: a provider `end_turn` is not authority to complete this workflow. The host accepts success only from the exact `earnings-research` renderer trace (`success=true`, `render_success=true`, `side_effect_status=completed`, nonempty trusted `validated_report_markdown`, `application/pdf` document) plus a PDF actually collected/persisted into the Web response. The trusted `skill_tool` copies this Markdown from the successful script payload into its compact result because ACP call arguments are not a stable terminal trace. Once renderer success is observed, Hone deterministically replaces model terminal prose with that exact validated Markdown and its artifact marker; a missing filename therefore cannot trigger a second full-report generation or consume the remaining deadline. Renderer preflight returns all ordinary errors in one priority-ordered response and caps only pathological sets at 32, avoiding the production-observed 20→12→6→single-item feedback staircase. OpenCode's post-compaction same-session completion prompts are disabled because the real AAOI trace accepted all three as zero-token no-ops. If an incomplete attempt contains only known read-only calls plus exact renderer rejections with `side_effect_status=not_started`, empty artifacts and a concrete validation error, Hone abandons the exhausted ACP process and rebuilds the isolated current turn once. Any artifact, unknown call or unknown/write side-effect state remains fail-closed. Earnings answer deltas stay deferred until this terminal contract passes, so a model-authored “PDF rendering failed” paragraph cannot flash or persist as success.
- OpenCode result-envelope consequence: compatible OpenCode ACP `1.18.13` may carry MCP `rawOutput.output` as a JSON string rather than an object even though the tool update itself is `completed`. The OpenCode runner must parse a valid embedded JSON value before creating the terminal tool trace. Otherwise both safe renderer rejection fields and successful PDF artifacts become opaque, causing a false uncertain-side-effect failure. Non-JSON strings retain their original text semantics.
- Gemini-signature recovery consequence: OpenRouter/Gemini can occasionally reject the next tool round with the exact `400 invalid_request: Corrupted thought signature` protocol error even though the dedicated turn already excluded durable chat history. This is not accepted as a report/PDF failure and is not handled by weakening reasoning or renderer validation. Only the server-verified OpenCode earnings route may recover it, only when the exact structured signature error is present, no visible prefix was committed, every observed call is known read-only and no persistent side effect is present. Hone then abandons that ephemeral OpenCode session, rebuilds the already-isolated current turn once, and retries under the normal run boundary. A second occurrence, a fuzzy/internal error, another runner, ordinary chat, an unknown/write tool, or an uncertain side effect remains fail-closed.
- Upstream-idle recovery consequence: the same one-time fresh-session rule also covers only OpenCode/OpenRouter's exact structured `504` with message `Upstream idle timeout exceeded` and `error_type=timeout`. This is narrower than generic timeouts and is allowed only for the dedicated earnings route before visible output when every observed call is known read-only and no artifact or persistent/unknown side effect exists. A fuzzy gateway/timeout message, another status/type, a second occurrence, ordinary chat, or any unsafe trace remains fail-closed.
- Validator-feedback consequence: renderer failures intended for model self-repair must name the accepted literal or field shape, not only an abstract English requirement. The report continues to be rejected until it uses the Workflow term `中性带` (or another explicitly accepted token), but the tool response now tells the runner exactly which literal is valid so strictness does not create a synonym retry loop.
- Language boundary: validation protects semantic fields and audited numbers, not one stylistic synonym. `收入` and standard financial Chinese `营收` are equivalent for the revenue-field presence check; missing-field errors enumerate both accepted literals plus the other exact required tokens. Arithmetic, units, headings, evidence families, news freshness, and call consistency remain strict.
- Verification: authenticated OpenRouter probes returned HTTP 200 from Google and the exact response model. Focused config/Web/runner/execution/session tests pin the exact model, OpenRouter prefix, global-Codex-to-OpenCode switch, ephemeral compiled prompt ownership, structured script payload, institution/news validation and non-admin rejection. The `skill-creator` validator and complete repository gates pass. Production runs binary revision `2c2cd1db` with the `105ca177` skill/renderer; the final AAOI session recorded `providerID=openrouter` and `modelID=google/gemini-3.1-pro-preview`, compared Rosenblatt and Needham, rendered an eight-paragraph company-relevant news page and a four-page PDF with canonical `USD millions` audit, exact watermark/share image, and a download card that survived page refresh.

## D-2026-08-06-01 Answer Breadth Is An Evidence-Supply Problem, Not A Prompting One
- Status: Implemented on `main`, not deployed.
- Problem: a competitor report on the same SanDisk quarter was materially more useful than Hone's, and the gap was not analytical skill. Hone's headline figures were all correct — revenue, gross margin, both EPS lines, the full-year totals — but the answer had no sequential comparison, no segment split, no cash flow and no balance-sheet context, because `financials` fetched only four annual income statements. `/v3/income-statement?limit=4` returns annual periods; nothing in the pipeline ever fetched a quarterly statement, a cash-flow statement or a balance sheet. Two instructions then compounded it: the finance research budget of three rounds is spent confirming identity and a price, and both the terminal contract and the finish-research instruction asked for a "最小充分" final answer, so the turn was told to be brief about evidence it had.
- Decision: treat breadth as supply, not style. `financials` becomes a bundle — annual income statement (unchanged under `data`, so existing consumers are untouched), quarterly income statement, quarterly balance sheet, quarterly cash flow — with `hone_ttm` summed from the four filed quarters and `hone_latest_quarter` carrying sequential change, year-ago change, gross margin and operating cash flow. The pre-turn pass fetches it for every uniquely resolved security in the same parallel stage as the snapshot and extended-hours calls, so the model holds fundamentals before it starts thinking. The research budget rises from three rounds to five. The two minimality instructions are rewritten to restrain claim strength rather than coverage, keeping every anti-overclaim rule intact.
- Coverage consequence: the gap list in the normalized financial evidence was a fixed constant asserting that cash flow, free cash flow, capital expenditure, cash, debt and net debt were unverified. With those statements now fetched, that constant would have told the model to disclaim evidence it was holding — the same "answer narrower than the evidence" defect class as the pre-market quote. The list is now derived from `hone_statement_coverage`, and the unverified branch declares an empty scope instead of naming a statement it never checked.
- Valuation-basis consequence: both reports on that quarter published a trailing P/E of roughly 46x. The correct figure against the just-filed year was about 18x; the provider's `eps`/`pe` still covered the pre-release window, and the competitor invented an explanation for the stale number rather than detecting it. `hone_valuation_basis` compares the provider's trailing EPS with `hone_ttm`, marks whether the provider window includes the latest filed quarter, publishes a recomputed EPS and P/E, and quarantines multiple claims when they disagree. This is structural and generalizes to every ticker in every earnings season; it is not a rule about one company.
- Caching consequence: the four statement components keep the `financials` cache label. The caching policy refuses to memoize an empty payload for that data type, and a component-specific label would have silently opted out of the rule — an empty statement would have been cached for the full TTL.
- Verification: `hone-tools` 179, `hone-channels` 779, `hone-agent` 148 pass. New coverage: the bundle's trailing window and quarter-over-quarter arithmetic, quarantine of a stale provider multiple, an unverifiable basis reported as unverified rather than confirmed, coverage-derived gap lists, and an end-to-end assertion that quarterly statements, the trailing window, margins and cash flow actually reach the runtime context. Acceptance contract 45 locks all of it and was confirmed to fail when the valuation basis or trailing-window helpers are removed.

## D-2026-08-06-02 Offer Recurring And Fixed-term Stripe Memberships As Separate Products

- title: Offer recurring and fixed-term Stripe memberships as separate products
- status: `deployed`; live wallet availability blocked on Stripe approval
- created_at: `2026-08-06`
- updated_at: `2026-08-06`
- owner: `Codex + owner`
- related_files: `memory/src/billing.rs`, `crates/hone-core/src/cloud_runtime.rs`, `crates/hone-web-api/src/routes/{billing,stripe}.rs`, `packages/app/src/pages/{public-activate,public-me}.tsx`
- related_docs: `docs/current-plans/stripe-wallet-one-time-pass.md`, `docs/runbooks/stripe-billing.md`
- supersedes: subscription-shaped assumptions inside `D-2026-08-04-01`; Stripe-only provider authority remains unchanged
- superseded_by:

### Context

Stripe's US account can sell a USD one-time product through card, Alipay, and
WeChat Pay, but those wallets are not the renewal rail for HONE's existing
subscription Checkout. Treating a PaymentIntent as a Subscription would make
expiration, refund, Portal, and out-of-order webhook behavior ambiguous. The
owner chose a clean cut: preserve USD 199.99/year auto-renewal and add a
separate USD 229.99/12-month non-renewing pass, without compatibility code or
a user-controlled catalog.

### Decision

Stripe remains the sole external payment authority, while the Billing ledger
stores two explicit entitlement kinds. A `recurring_subscription` is keyed by
Subscription ID and retains Invoice payment, renewal grace, cancellation, and
Portal semantics. A `fixed_term_purchase` is keyed by PaymentIntent ID, starts
only after a verified paid Checkout event, ends exactly 12 calendar months
later, never receives grace, and has no Portal action. The server owns both
Price IDs, amounts, currency, and term; the client selects only
`subscription` or `fixed_term`.

The one-time offer uses `mode=payment` with Stripe dynamic payment methods so
the account's Payment Method Configuration can expose card, Alipay, and WeChat
Pay. Completed-but-unpaid, failed, expired, wrong-mode, wrong-catalog, and
forged events fail closed. A full Charge refund revokes only the entitlement
whose PaymentIntent matches that Charge. Partial refund and dispute policy
remain owner decisions and cannot silently alter unrelated access.

### Consequences

SQLite and PostgreSQL migrate the old `provider_subscription_id` shape to
`provider_reference_id`, backfill every existing Stripe row as recurring, and
admit typed fixed-term rows without a dual-read. Public Billing responses expose
kind and server-derived access state. `/activate` presents two independently
priced offers; `/me` shows a fixed validity date and never sends a fixed-term
buyer to Customer Portal. Both kinds may coexist, and access is the OR of their
independent valid rows.

### Verification / Adoption

Storage and Stripe unit tests cover migration, leap-day term calculation,
pending/paid events, replay, full versus partial refund, and kind/reference
isolation. Signed-event HTTP E2E additionally proves that unpaid Checkout grants
nothing, a paid one-time event grants once, replay cannot extend it, full refund
revokes only that pass, and an independent subscription continues to grant.
Official Stripe test Checkout has now completed both an Alipay and WeChat Pay
test payment at USD 229.99, and the complete repository gates pass. The live
one-time Price exists as `price_1U1M0rEK7h1dD4JHbKBpIkZ2`; the production
webhook destination listens to the exact ten-event contract. Stripe has
accepted the live enablement requests but still reports both wallets as
`pending approval` (`display_preference=on`, `available=false`). Exact revision
`c99babc1e1ea3c54db41256331eb65dcefa7bd1d` is deployed from immutable GHCR
digest `sha256:dadf8fcf340cf8fa4971605c3f085f7e097efc7cc2c9a8e1ff4a61d757ca90cb`.
External Chrome confirmed both production offers and a USD 229.99 fixed-term
live Checkout without submitting money. The live form currently exposes card
only, which matches Stripe's pending state; wallet-visible production
acceptance remains an external Stripe approval gate rather than application
implementation work.
## D-2026-08-06-03 Comparisons Need One Window, And Forward Multiples Need A Source
- Status: Implemented on `main`, not deployed.
- Problem: a memory/storage comparison published Micron on FY2025 (ended 2025-08-29) beside SanDisk, Western Digital and Seagate on FY2026 (ended around 2026-07). Each margin was individually correct and the table was still wrong: a full year of cycle position separated the rows, and Micron's own most recent quarter already showed 52% gross margin against the 39.8% FY2025 figure in the table. The multiples inherited the same defect — a 19.8x trailing P/E on a trough-year EPS beside a 43.4x on a peak-year EPS compares nothing — and a market cap computed from a current price was divided by a two-year-old annual EPS. Separately, `forward PE`/`forward PS` had no source at all: nothing in the pipeline fetched or computed them, so a forward comparison was impossible even though it is the right one for a cyclical industry. Finally, SK Hynix and Samsung had no provider coverage, and the turn hedged them into near-uselessness rather than publishing attributed public figures.
- Decision: give every comparison a single window and give forward multiples a real source. `hone_ttm` gains gross and operating margin, making it a complete same-window basis independent of fiscal calendars. `financials` additionally fetches analyst estimates and derives `hone_forward` — the four estimate periods dated strictly after the latest filed quarter, with `min_analyst_count`. `valuation_basis_quality` becomes public and derives `forward_pe` and `forward_ps` alongside the existing trailing-window quarantine, and the pre-turn pass calls it directly, because that is the one place where the quote and the statements are both in hand.
- Forward-window consequence: "forward" is defined by the reported window, not by a clock. The provider's estimate series contains already-reported periods, and using a server clock to split them would disagree with the filings the same turn is holding. Fewer than four forward periods yields no summary at all rather than a three-quarter number labelled as twelve months.
- Comparison consequence: the enrichment block instructs cross-company tables to land on `hone_ttm` or `hone_forward` with the period ends printed, and forbids mixing per-company fiscal-year trailing multiples and margins. This is a window rule, not a company rule; it holds for any industry whose members report on different calendars.
- Coverage consequence: the answer contract now states what to do when the provider does not cover a named security. Public search figures are publishable with source name, URL and as-of date, explicitly labelled as public-source rather than provider quotes, excluded from the `行情口径：` first line, and never mixed into a provider-quote column without that distinction. Dropping the security, or answering 无法核验, is not an acceptable substitute — the user asked about it.
- Non-decision: no exchange allowlist was added or removed. The three existing lists were checked and none of them filters non-US listings — one decides whether US extended-hours windows apply, one is a scheduled-origin token list, and one is a +20 search-ranking tiebreaker. A missing Korean listing is provider plan coverage or a query that used a name instead of the local symbol, not a Hone gate.
- Verification: `hone-tools` 180, `hone-channels` 779, `hone-agent` 148 pass. New coverage: forward-window selection excluding already-reported periods and withholding short windows, forward P/E and P/S arithmetic, and an end-to-end assertion that `hone_forward` and a server-computed `hone_valuation_basis` reach the runtime context with the stale-trailing warning intact. Acceptance contract 46 locks it and was confirmed to fail both when the forward summary is removed and when the same-window instruction is weakened.

## D-2026-08-06-04 Declare The Provider Surface Instead Of Discovering It From Wrong Answers
- Status: Implemented on `main`, not deployed.
- Problem: every earlier fix in this sequence closed exactly one gap, and each was found by a user hitting a wrong answer — no quarterly statements, then no forward estimates, then no comparable window. Reading the provider's documented surface showed why: FMP's stable API publishes roughly 250 endpoints and Hone called 19. Whole question classes had no source at all. Trailing ratios, ROE/ROIC, leverage and liquidity were being recomputed by hand from statements when the provider publishes them; enterprise value, Altman Z, Piotroski, DCF, share float, segment revenue by product and region, provider peer classification, industry P/E, multi-period price change, official aftermarket quotes, growth rates, price-target dispersion, rating distribution, institutional and insider ownership, dividends and splits, earnings-call transcripts, treasury rates, macro indicators and official exchange hours had none. Separately, `snapshot` was issuing its three calls sequentially for no reason.
- Decision: make the capability surface declarative. `stable_bundle_components` maps a data type to a list of `(key, path)` pairs, and one generic bundler fetches them concurrently, records `available` / `empty` / `unavailable` per component, and attaches per-component errors. Adding a capability is a row, not a dispatcher change — the structural reason gaps were closed one at a time. New types: `valuation`, `segments`, `peers`, `ownership`, `corporate_actions`, `press_releases`, `transcript`, `macro`, `market_hours`. Existing types gain what the provider already offered: `snapshot` adds multi-period price change and the official aftermarket quote and now runs in parallel; `extended_hours` adds the official aftermarket quote and trade beside Hone's own minute aggregation; `financials` adds growth; `earnings_outlook` adds price-target summary and rating distribution.
- Published-over-derived consequence: `valuation` returns the provider's own TTM key metrics and ratios rather than statement arithmetic. Hone's derived `hone_ttm` and `hone_valuation_basis` remain, but their job is to check whether a provider aggregate has caught up with the latest filing and to supply a comparable window — not to substitute for published figures.
- Score-semantics consequence: Altman Z and Piotroski arrive as bare numbers. `hone_score_semantics` attaches the published bands, the 0-9 scale, and Altman's limited applicability outside manufacturing, because a score without its threshold makes the model supply one from memory. A missing score yields a null band rather than a default that would read as a verdict.
- Anchor consequence: `peers` uses the provider's classification plus a batch quote and an industry P/E snapshot. "Is it expensive" needs a comparison the model did not select; peers recalled from memory are the same failure mode as a remembered ticker.
- Coverage consequence: `empty` and `unavailable` are distinct states. A component that returned nothing is not the same fact as one that failed, and neither may be published as "this company has none".
- Preloading consequence: `valuation` joins `financials` in the pre-turn pass under the same sub-deadline, so official multiples and health scores are in context before the model starts. `segments`, `peers`, `ownership` and the rest stay Agent-initiated — they are question-dependent, and the tool description now states when each applies.
- Verification: `hone-tools` 183, `hone-channels` 779, `hone-agent` 148 pass. New coverage: per-component availability including the `empty` vs `unavailable` distinction, Altman band boundaries at 1.81/2.99 including the null case, and symbol-independent bundles resolving without a ticker. The snapshot cache test now pins five parallel component calls. Acceptance contract 47 locks the surface and was confirmed to fail when the bundle table is renamed away.
## D-2026-08-06-06 Treat Earnings As Actor-Scoped Coverage Updates

- Status: Accepted for the first implementation slice; historical four-quarter continuity and a complete live earnings-season trial remain open under `docs/current-plans/institutional-company-coverage.md`.
- Product boundary: A reviewed earnings event is not a one-sentence news item. Its durable immediate contract includes a conclusion, up to three key facts, the strongest counterevidence, explicit unknowns, and named follow-up questions. Longer output is not itself success; each field must help the user decide what to verify next.
- Personalization boundary: Shared source facts and the model quality review remain event-level. Only after routing resolves the final actor may rendering read that actor's `mainline_by_ticker`. The stored mainline is shown as read-only comparison context and cannot be automatically strengthened, weakened, or replaced. Missing context produces an explicit generic fallback rather than simulated personalization.
- Timeliness boundary: Earnings discovery uses a dedicated interval during common US pre/post-market release windows. FMP SEC `acceptedDate` is interpreted as DST-aware US/Eastern and becomes the event timestamp when the selected 8-K/earnings release is the supporting source. Multiple recent 8-K candidates prefer an earnings-looking exhibit before recency.
- Notification boundary: A filing URL identified as an earnings/press-release document is supporting material for the structured earnings card and defaults to digest severity. Pollers, routing, delivery history, and digest buffering share one canonical document key. If the SEC item arrives first it remains a safety fallback until the actor actually receives the structured card; successful delivery removes that queued fallback and suppresses a later same-document SEC item. A failed or quiet-held structured send preserves the SEC item. A queued structured card may replace it inside the digest because that richer card remains pending rather than discarding the only delivery path. The structured card bypasses generic plain-text polishing so required research fields survive consistently across Plain, Discord, Telegram, and Feishu rendering.
- Delivery-truth boundary: A real channel send that fails may be copied to the diagnostic log sink, but that copy cannot acknowledge the original delivery. Actor-specific sink status distinguishes registered real delivery from an intentionally unregistered `dryrun` fallback, so router and digest history cannot record false `sent` rows or discard the only remaining SEC fallback.
- Restart boundary: The earnings poller may avoid repeating SEC/LLM review after restart only when the corresponding reviewed event is already durable. It does not populate the in-memory reviewed cache before pipeline persistence when a store is attached, so an insertion failure remains retryable.
- Research-memory boundary: New company portraits carry an A/B/C coverage tier and investment horizon and expose explicit expectation, valuation, management-commitment, catalyst, open-question, and decision-log sections. Existing portraits remain valid and default to C/long-term; this slice does not yet claim that commitments or questions are automatically reconciled across quarters.
- Model boundary: The immediate earnings-quality profile defaults to OpenRouter `x-ai/grok-4.5`, selected after a live 2026-08-06 comparison on SNDK, AMD, and BE. Model freshness is preferred within models that satisfy the source-bounded research contract, but newer release date or lower token price cannot override numerical fidelity. The prompt preserves source B/M units and limits consensus language to supplied actual/estimate pairs. At the observed average of about `$0.01309` per reviewed earnings release, inference cost is event-level rather than actor-level and therefore does not multiply with the number of subscribed users.
- Verification: Unit contracts cover the SNDK-shaped full research card, source-unit and consensus-language rules, US/Eastern conversion, release-window gating, earnings-document selection, duplicate 8-K downgrading, canonical document identity, both SEC/structured arrival orders, queued structured-card preference, cross-restart review short-circuit, insertion retry, failed-channel fallback retention, cross-format field preservation, generic fallback, and two actors with different mainlines and no cross-user leakage. The live model harness uses the production SEC extractor and prompt against public SNDK/AMD/BE exhibits and reports contract shape, latency, tokens, and price-derived cost without writing delivery state. Broader crate, workspace, frontend, Worker, CI-safe, and offline classifier gates are recorded in the linked handoff.

## D-2026-08-06-05 Persist Earnings Research Continuity As An Actor-Scoped Ledger

- Status: Accepted and implemented; full-transcript reconciliation, professional-investor UI, and a forward live earnings-season trial remain open.
- Product boundary: Retention comes from a professional investor's thesis, unresolved questions, management commitments, evidence, and decision history remaining in one company research object. A new earnings release updates that object; it is not a disconnected notification or a fresh generic report.
- Subscription boundary: Portfolio holdings and explicitly tracked company profiles jointly define event-engine subscriptions. A-tier profiles receive the full symbol event stream, B-tier profiles receive earnings/transcript/SEC/critical-news/corporate-action coverage with lower interruption severity, and C-tier profiles receive earnings-only discovery. Coverage tier is actor-scoped and cannot change another actor's delivery.
- Ledger boundary: Research questions and management commitments use stable IDs and append-only profile events. Their original kind and statement are immutable; later quarters may update status, assessment, evidence, and due date but may not silently rewrite the original research question. Missing model output carries the prior item forward rather than deleting it.
- Judgment boundary: The continuity model may propose `strengthen / unchanged / watch / weaken`, create research questions, and record explicit management commitments. It may not modify the saved investment mainline, valuation assumptions, disconfirming conditions, or coverage tier. Those remain user-confirmed state.
- Attention boundary: A company may retain at most eight active questions and six active commitments; each earnings cycle can add at most two of each. The system asks the model to reconcile every active item compactly, while deterministic normalization enforces product limits. This prevents the accumulated research asset from becoming an unbounded task list.
- Material boundary: A reviewed earnings release anchors one quarterly research object. A same-ticker transcript, 10-Q, or 10-K may join it only inside a 45-day disclosure window; without a reviewed anchor it remains unlinked rather than guessing a fiscal period. Follow-up materials are separate append-only evidence events marked pending cross-check, and their archival step spends no model tokens or ledger authority.
- Delivery boundary: A-tier continuity runs only after the actor's T0 earnings notification is admitted and is scheduled in the background. It cannot delay the immediate fact card. Actor plus research-object identity makes the update idempotent across restart and repeated dispatch.
- Recovery boundary: A-tier continuity is a SQLite-backed job, not a best-effort process-local spawn. Pending, retry, and expired-running jobs are reclaimed with a fifteen-minute lease and bounded exponential backoff. Completion and retry writes must match the claimed attempt, so a stale worker cannot overwrite a replacement worker after lease expiry. A coverage downgrade closes an old job without model work; a model response that produced no durable profile event is not acknowledged as complete.
- Model boundary: Immediate fact extraction and actor continuity use independent OpenRouter providers and completion budgets even when both resolve to `x-ai/grok-4.5`. The short fact card retains a 1,800-token budget; multi-quarter continuity uses 3,600 tokens with low reasoning effort. Historical ledger evidence is preserved durably but the model input includes only each active item's current compact state. This avoids both cost growth and truncated JSON after several quarters.
- Numerical boundary: Financial-table values must preserve source B/M units. A comma-formatted value such as `$13,335B` is rejected as numerically unsafe; a source table marked `in millions` must remain `$13,335M` or be correctly converted to `$13.335B`. Model-authored arrays and headlines are deterministically trimmed to the user-facing contract before routing.
- Verification boundary: A repository fixture covers six company archetypes and four consecutive quarters each using 24 unique first-party SEC exhibit URLs. Offline tests prove fixture shape, append-only identity, carry-forward behavior, restart idempotency, coverage-tier subscription cadence, non-blocking T0 delivery, transcript/formal-filing object linkage in both arrival orders even behind 250 unrelated same-time events, zero-token material archival, lease expiry recovery, stale-attempt rejection, retry, and completion. The opt-in manual replay fetches the real SEC exhibits and executes the production quality plus continuity contracts against the configured OpenRouter model.

## D-2026-08-06-07 Ship The User Survey Without A Login, And Without Storing Who Answered
- Status: Implemented on `main`, not deployed. Schema creation is automatic; see `docs/runbooks/user-survey.md`.
- Problem: product direction was being argued from anecdote. A survey needs to reach people who are not logged in — including the ones who churned, who are exactly the most informative respondents — so requiring an account would bias the sample toward the users who are already happy.
- Decision: a public `/survey` page and a `POST /api/public/survey` endpoint with no authentication, plus an admin-only read endpoint. Responses land in a new `survey_responses` table in cloud mode and an append-only JSONL file in local mode.
- Question-bank consequence: the bank lives only in the frontend content tree (ZH and EN, held identical by the existing shape-parity test and by `CONTENT_EN: typeof CONTENT_ZH`). The backend treats answers as opaque JSON and validates structure only — key charset and length, question count, option count, value type. Changing or adding a question therefore touches neither the backend nor the database. The cost is that the backend cannot reject a key the frontend never sent, which the charset and count caps bound.
- Anonymity consequence: no raw IP and no User-Agent is stored. Duplicate detection uses a salted digest, and the salt must be secret — an unsalted hash of an IP is not anonymous, since the whole IPv4 space enumerates in seconds. `HONE_SURVEY_DIGEST_SALT` is required for multi-replica deployments; without it each replica generates its own salt and the 24-hour duplicate window silently stops working. `client_digest` is not a field on the read model at all, so no reader can leak it by accident.
- Abuse consequence: three layers — a per-IP cooldown, a per-IP hourly in-process quota, and a durable per-digest count. Only the last survives a restart or spans replicas; the first two are fast rejections, not guarantees. A failure to run the durable count admits the response and warns, because losing a genuine answer is worse than accepting a duplicate.
- Survey-design consequence: the draft asked respondents to self-rate as 经常/偶尔. Subjective adverbs vary several-fold between people and produce unusable buckets, so Q1 uses behavioural anchors over a recallable window (days opened in the last 30) and adds a "used it, but not in the last 30 days" option — that separates churned users from light users, two groups whose needs differ completely. The draft's tool question was a matrix ("豆包 — 功能？"), which is slow to fill and sparse to analyze; it became a multi-select plus one free-text follow-up.
- Usability consequence: answers are drafted to `localStorage` on every change and restored for a week, because a twelve-question form loses people to an accidental refresh. Touch targets are 44px on phones — below that a mis-tap silently changes an answer, and there are about ninety chips to get through. A multi-select at its limit ignores the extra pick rather than dropping an earlier one, which would look like a checkbox unticking itself elsewhere on the page.
- Verification: `hone-core` 136, `hone-memory` 140, `hone-web-api` 191, `hone-channels` 779 pass; `bunx tsc --noEmit` clean; frontend suite unchanged at its 9 pre-existing DOMPurify/Playwright failures. Browser-verified on desktop and at 375px: rendering, option selection, progress, draft restore across a navigation, no horizontal overflow, all 92 tap targets ≥44px, and the sticky progress bar clearing the fixed nav.

## D-2026-08-06-08 Review Transcripts Once, Then Reconcile Actor Research State

- Status: Accepted and implemented for source-backed replay and reviewed-event handling; automated production source acquisition remains open.
- Source boundary: A full earnings-call transcript is fetched from a first-party company IR URL into ephemeral memory. The shared reviewer receives the body once and emits a compact object separating prepared remarks, analyst questions, direct/partial/evaded answers, explicit commitments, contradictions, and unresolved questions. Transcript bodies are not committed, printed, or persisted. A provider snippet under 2,000 characters is rejected rather than treated as a transcript.
- Cost and personalization boundary: Full-source fact extraction is event-level and does not repeat per subscriber. Only the compact fact object enters an A-tier actor reconciliation with that actor's saved thesis and ledger. Release and transcript reconciliations share a quarterly research-object key but have distinct durable profile-event and job stages, so one cannot incorrectly deduplicate the other.
- Identity boundary: An FMP transcript article whose title contains an explicit `(TICKER)` immediately before its fiscal-period marker must match the payload symbol. A conflict is dropped; titles without an explicit ticker remain eligible and are not resolved by model memory or company-name guessing.
- Ledger-state boundary: `open_question` and `management_commitment` use different closure vocabularies. Every non-open change requires a matching `resolution_basis` and nonempty evidence. A reaffirmed or on-track future commitment remains open; only an explicitly fulfilled result may become confirmed. Questions cannot use confirmed, commitments cannot use answered, and incompatible model output retains the prior status while preserving the assessment for audit. An `open` proposal also preserves any existing partial progress instead of regressing the ledger.
- Verification: The URL-only fixture covers AMD, Microsoft, Qualcomm, and Caterpillar across two calls each, including PDF and DOCX sources, and contains no transcript body. Two full `x-ai/grok-4.5` replays each passed 8/8 at 15/15; the first used 122,723 prompt and 21,512 completion tokens for about `$0.374518`, and the auditable-ledger replay used 122,736/22,946 for about `$0.383148`. Manual inspection found the premature Venice confirmation, after which deterministic state-basis guards were added; an AMD-only replay passed 2/2 for about `$0.092314` and kept reaffirmed future milestones open. The current FMP plan rejects the official transcript endpoint and its `stock_news` text is only a short excerpt, so no production full-source poller is claimed until a licensed sustainable source is selected.

## D-2026-08-07-01 Render Units Server-Side And Search Again Under The Verified Identity
- Status: Implemented on `main`, not deployed.
- Problem: an NBIS answer had two independent defects. It published a market capitalization of 4557 亿美元 for a company worth about 45.57bn — 455.7 亿, exactly ten times smaller — because converting a raw provider integer into Chinese magnitudes was left to prose.
