And it named a Vineland, New Jersey data-center zoning hearing as the core reason for a 13.29% drop. That citation was faithful: the article it quoted really is only about Vineland. The actual driver was Michael Burry disclosing a short at $211.77 that same day, reported in English, which the turn never saw. The pre-turn search runs in the same stage as identity resolution, so its only possible query is the user's own words — here a Chinese sentence — and it reached Chinese-language coverage of a secondary story. Two independent-looking sources both described that story, so the model converged on it confidently.
- Decision: move both jobs off the model. `hone_display` renders market cap and share count once, in the listing's own currency, into 万 / 亿 / 万亿, and the answer contract requires copying that string rather than converting a raw field. After the registry confirms an identity, the pre-turn pass issues a second, identity-anchored search built from the standard symbol, the registry's company name and the New York trading date, bounded to two queries and running in the same parallel stage as the fundamentals calls.
- Magnitude consequence: each Chinese unit step is a factor of 10_000, so a value that rounds to 10000.00 in one unit is really 1.00 of the next. Printing "10000.00 万" reads as 一亿 and defeats the purpose, so the renderer promotes across the boundary. This was found by the test written for the fix, not in review.
- Attribution consequence: breadth of evidence does not by itself prevent a premature conclusion, so the contract also states the rule directly. A single source naming a single cause may not be published as the core reason; the answer discloses the other same-period items found and says when only one source supports a cause. A day carries several distinct stories and the first one read is not the largest one.
- Non-decision: no keyword list, source allowlist or per-topic query template was added. The second query is derived entirely from what the registry returned — symbol, name, date — so it generalizes to any listing in any language, and a registry row whose name equals its symbol degrades to a symbol-only query rather than stuttering.
- Verification: `hone-tools` 184, `hone-channels` 782, `hone-agent` 148 pass. New coverage: magnitude boundaries at 1e4/1e8/1e12 including the promotion case, negative values, non-USD and unmapped currencies; identity-anchored query construction including the name-equals-symbol and empty-name cases and the provider length bound; and an end-to-end NBIS turn asserting the rendered 455.70 亿美元 reaches the runtime context while 4557 亿 does not. Acceptance contract 48 locks both halves and was confirmed to fail when either the renderer or the query builder is removed.

## D-2026-08-07-02 Fix Perceived Latency Where It Occurs Rather Than By Doing Less Work
- Status: Implemented on `main`, not deployed.
- Problem: users reported that clicking a section had a noticeable delay before it switched, and that a sent message sat silent for a long time before anything appeared. Three separate causes. Every route is `lazy()`, so the first navigation to one downloaded its chunk before it could render anything. The account page then called `/api/public/auth/me` from zero and replaced the entire viewport with a full-screen loading state, even when the chat it was opened from had already resolved that same user — the navigation was instant and the blank screen was the whole perceived delay. And the pre-turn evidence pass, which this sequence of changes grew to roughly twenty provider calls, emitted exactly two progress lines for the entire window.
- Decision: fix each where it occurs rather than by reducing the work. Route chunks are warmed on pointer entry, focus and touch start, all of which precede activation by enough time to hide the download; the import is idempotent so repeated hovering costs nothing. A module-level session cache holds the resolved user, written by `getPublicAuthMe` and read as the initial value by the account page, which now paints immediately and revalidates behind the visible content. The pre-turn pass takes an optional progress sink and reports `preturn.identity` and `preturn.evidence` with the tokens and resolved symbols involved; the session drains that channel concurrently with the pass via `tokio::select!`, so stages surface while the work is still running rather than after it.
- Non-decision: the research budget was not reduced and no preloaded capability was removed. The depth added in this sequence is what the answers needed; the defect was that the wait was illegible, not that the work was wrong. Making the wait visible and naming the securities being read is the fix.
- Cache-invalidation consequence: a session cache that outlives the session is worse than the loading state it replaced, because it paints a signed-out visitor as signed in. It is cleared on sign-out and on a failed revalidation, and stores a copy so a later mutation of the caller's object cannot rewrite it.
- Detail-safety consequence: stage details are server-authored but still reach the browser. Only alphanumerics (which covers CJK) and the punctuation that occurs in symbols and company names survive, bounded to 60 characters; `/` is kept because provider symbols use it. A stage with no usable detail falls back to its detail-free wording rather than rendering an empty slot.
- Verification: `hone-channels` 782, `hone-web-api` 197 pass; frontend `bun test` delta is zero against the 9 pre-existing failures (a DOMPurify environment issue in the Markdown suite). Prefetch was verified empirically in the browser: calling it fetched `pages/public-me.tsx` and `pages/public-community.tsx` and a repeat call issued no second request. New coverage: session cache lifecycle including copy-on-write and sign-out clearing, stage wording with and without a detail in both languages, and detail sanitizing against markup. `tests/regression/ci/test_navigation_responsiveness_contract.sh` locks all three fixes and was confirmed to fail when the prefetch trigger is removed.

## D-2026-08-07-03 Budget Each Evidence Branch Separately, And Repaint Before Revalidating
- Status: Implemented on `main`, not deployed.
- Problem: two leftovers from the responsiveness work. The pre-turn pass wrapped every provider call in one 12-second timeout, so a single slow branch discarded the whole pass — including identity searches and quotes that had already returned — and the turn continued with no preloaded evidence at all. That is the worst possible failure for a pass whose entire purpose is to have evidence in hand before the model starts. Separately, the community section still showed a loading line on every visit even when the page it had shown moments earlier was perfectly readable.
- Decision: budget by phase. Identity resolution gates everything downstream, so it gets its own short clock and returns nothing when it misses — there is no useful work to do without a confirmed symbol. Every evidence branch after it is bounded individually, so a slow provider degrades that branch to "nothing fetched" while the branches that completed still reach the turn. The community feed joins the session cache: the previous page repaints immediately and revalidates behind itself, a refresh that fails does not replace content already on screen, and an unauthorized response clears the cache so a signed-out visitor stops reading it.
- Not done: `deterministic_non_security_token` remains. Removing it was attempted and measured — dropping the list breaks three tests, two of which cover legitimate scheduled tickers resolving from a subject line, so the list is load-bearing beyond blocking generic acronyms. It applies only to non-Interactive origins, where no Agent reads the request, and replacing it properly means routing scheduled entity resolution through provider lookups — a refactor of an unattended path that should not ride along with a responsiveness change.
- Verification: `hone-channels` 785 pass; frontend contract tests cover the community repaint, the failed-refresh path and cache clearing on sign-out. `test_navigation_responsiveness_contract.sh` was extended and confirmed to fail when the per-branch bound is removed.

## D-2026-08-07-04 One Token Vocabulary, And A Theme That Follows The System By Default
- Status: Implemented on `main`, not deployed.
- Problem: a whole-product sweep of the public surface found the interface split across two token vocabularies. Seven `--hone-*` names were referenced but defined nowhere — six of them on the survey page, which had been written against `--hone-surface`, `--hone-text`, `--hone-text-strong`, `--hone-text-muted`, `--hone-border` and `--hone-accent`. Each reference silently fell through to a hardcoded fallback authored for the opposite theme, so the page never responded to the theme at all: measured in dark mode, its `h1` rendered at a contrast ratio of 1.01 and every question heading at 1.19 — both invisible — while its option chips stayed bright white against a dark page. Separately, the theme preference defaulted to `light` rather than `auto`, so a first-time visitor on a dark machine got a light page with nothing on screen explaining why, even though the interface language had already been made to follow the system. And `public-survey.css` was the only file keying its dark rules off `prefers-color-scheme` while the other eight used `[data-theme="dark"]`, meaning that one surface could disagree with the user's explicit choice.
- Decision: collapse to one vocabulary and one switch. The phantom names are rewritten to the tokens that actually exist and flip under `[data-theme="dark"]` — `--hone-surface-raised`, `--hone-ink-950`, `--hone-ink-600`, `--hone-paper-200`, `--hone-coral-500`, `--hone-error-600` — and their stale fallbacks are dropped, since a defined token never needs one and the old values were written for the wrong theme. The survey's `prefers-color-scheme` block becomes a `[data-theme="dark"]` rule. The theme default becomes `auto`, matching the language preference; the existing system-change listener already kept `auto` live, so only the default was wrong.
- Silent-failure consequence: this class cannot be caught by review, because the CSS parses and the page renders — only the colour is wrong, and only in one theme. A contract test now asserts that every referenced `--hone-*` name is defined and that no surface outside the preference resolver reads `prefers-color-scheme`.
- Not done: the workspace and chat surfaces still carry roughly 1,300 hardcoded colour literals against 34 tokens, and they are a cool-grey palette while the tokens are warm paper-and-ink — two different palettes, not one palette written twice. Migrating them is a visual redesign of the product's main surface, not a consistency fix, and it needs the owner's judgement on how the result should look rather than a mechanical substitution. The admin console is a third system again, styled with Tailwind utilities including the amber the public palette retired; it is a separate internal surface and was left alone.
- Verification: measured in the browser before and after. Dark mode: `h1` 1.01 → 16.0, question heading 1.19 → 13.63, supporting text 3.09 → 7.71. Light mode: every measured pair ≥ 5.47, so nothing regressed on the side that already worked. Both themes now clear WCAG AA. `tests/regression/ci/test_design_system_contract.sh` locks it and was confirmed to fail when a single phantom token is reintroduced.

## D-2026-08-07-05 Hand The Judgement To The Reader Instead Of To A Word List
- Status: Implemented on `main`, not deployed.
- Problem: `deterministic_non_security_token` was the last hand-kept vocabulary in the entity scanner — 136 acronyms that scheduled and heartbeat work consulted to decide whether a bare token was a security. Read in full it turned out to conflate five unrelated concerns: financial and hardware acronyms (PE, EBITDA, GPU), English stopwords (THE, AND, OF, IS), scheduler command words (MONITOR, DAILY, REPEAT), developer terms (API, JSON, SQL, OPENAI, CODEX), and trading slang (BUY, HOLD, BULL). Worse, eleven entries are real US listings, so a scheduled task saying `每天盯一下 ARM 的财报` lost ARM before anything could verify it. Measured directly: ARM, NOW, ON, AA, BE, IT and BB were all dropped while NVDA and CRWV survived, the only difference being list membership.
- Investigation: the obvious replacement — ask the registry instead of a list — does not work on its own, because AI (C3.ai), IT (Gartner) and ON (ON Semiconductor) all resolve to live listings, so the registry would confirm exactly the tokens the list exists to reject. Instrumenting the scanner's own binding signals showed why: `ARM 的财报` and `AI 板块` produce identical flags (`clause_subject_binding` only), and so does `NVDA 的财报`. No grammar signal available here separates them. The judgement requires reading the sentence, which is precisely what the interactive path delegates to the Agent and the scheduled path did not have.
- Decision: stop deciding. Every structural candidate survives the scan. A candidate that nothing in the sentence binds to a security — no exact-subject match, no ticker label, no explicit binding, no direct market binding — is marked tentative on non-interactive origins, and a tentative mention blocks the deterministic-contract scope, so the request goes to Agent discovery with its seeds. That is the same path interactive turns already take, where the discovery block tells the Agent these seeds come from weak grammar and must not be assumed to be securities. Nothing is erased and nothing is fabricated; the request simply gets the reader it was missing.
- Scope-boundary consequence: the tentative check lives at the `extract_entity_scope` call site rather than inside `deterministic_ticker_scope_is_complete`, because that helper is shared with the interactive path where tentative mentions are normal and load-bearing. Putting it in the helper broke lowercase interactive tickers such as `rklb`, which is how the boundary was found.
- Retained behaviour: an explicitly bound subject still settles. Scheduled work that writes `股票代码: RKLB` keeps its deterministic contract, so the unattended safety property — never push about a security the request did not name — is preserved by binding rather than by vocabulary.
- Verification: `hone-channels` 787, `hone-tools` 184, `hone-agent` 148, `hone-web-api` 197 pass. New coverage asserts all eleven real listings survive a scheduled scan, reach the Agent as candidates rather than decided entities, and route to discovery instead of a deterministic contract; a companion test pins that an explicitly labelled subject still settles. Two existing tests that asserted the erased tokens vanish were rewritten to assert the stronger property — they survive as tentative and produce no deterministic contract. Acceptance contract 49 locks it and was confirmed to fail when the tentative marking is removed.

## D-2026-08-07-06 Keep The Progress Trail Across A Refresh
- Status: Implemented on `main`, not deployed.
- Problem: a turn's visible progress trail — identity verified, quotes read, statements read — was rebuilt from `ActiveChatRun`, which stored only the single newest `status_text`. Refreshing mid-run therefore collapsed the trail to one line and the recovered card rendered with `steps: []`, so a turn that had done real work looked like it had forgotten what it was doing. This got worse as the pre-turn pass grew to roughly twenty provider calls and the research budget to five rounds: the trail is now most of what tells someone the wait is productive.
- Decision: the registry keeps the stages themselves. `ActiveChatRun` carries a bounded `steps` list using the same de-duplication and six-entry cap the client already applied to live events, so a recovered trail reads exactly like the one that was on screen. Recovery reads that list instead of discarding it. Heartbeats deliberately do not append: they prove the run is alive, not that it moved on, and a trail full of "still working" would be worse than none.
- Non-change: the durability model was audited and left alone, because it already holds. The run is `tokio::spawn`ed independently of the SSE response, every push is a discarded-result send so a disconnected client cannot abort it, and the turn is persisted by the session layer regardless of whether a listener remains. The registry is intentionally in-process: after a restart there is no run to recover, and the client reports the turn as interrupted rather than showing a thinking state that can never end.
- One-run-per-session: confirmed deliberate and left as is. Two concurrent runs would write the same conversation from two places; the second attempt is refused with a stated, localized reason rather than queued.
- Verification: `hone-web-api` 200 pass, including that a run accumulates its stages, that a repeated status is not a new stage, that a long run keeps only the most recent six, and that heartbeats leave the trail untouched. Client-side recovery is covered for a full trail, a server that sends none, blank and repeated entries, the bounded case, the interrupted case, and the empty case. `test_navigation_responsiveness_contract.sh` locks both halves and was confirmed to fail when either the server-side trail or the client-side recovery is removed.

## D-2026-08-10-01 Load Hari Invest Before Every Investment Answer

- Status: Accepted and implemented locally; not publicly released or deployed to production.
- Decision: company, security, ETF, industry, macro, market, fundamental, moat, valuation, portfolio, position, investment-review and investment-rating questions must load the public-facing `hari-invest` Skill before the final answer. Native Codex runners use their native projected Skill mechanism; function-calling runners use the real `skill_tool(skill_name="hari-invest")` path. Merely saying the framework was used is not sufficient.
- Evidence boundary: `hari-invest` owns the judgment framework and output discipline, not current facts. Current price, filing, news, valuation inputs, industry state and portfolio data continue to come from the existing turn-scoped HONE evidence tools. Answers distinguish verified facts, Hari-confirmed logic, AI inference and unknowns, and include the strongest contrary evidence and falsifiers.
- Composition boundary: `company-thesis-ratings` may be loaded alongside Hari Invest for companies covered by the private transcript-derived cards. The card is company-specific research context; Hari Invest is the common first-principles, scarcity/differentiation, market-state, portfolio and falsification layer. Current verifiable evidence and the more specific failure condition win any conflict.
- Privacy and scope boundary: obvious non-investment questions do not load the Skill. The internal `laowang-investment-distiller`, source transcripts, account information and other private distillation materials are never runtime Q&A skills. The 2026-08-10 authorization covers this local HONE installation only and does not authorize public distribution or production deployment.
- Compatibility consequence: the six confirmed `LOG-V0001..LOG-V0006` summaries live in the main `SKILL.md`, not only in references, because the function-calling path injects the main Skill body while native runners can progressively read references.
- Verification: Skill validator passed; prompt tests 14/14; discover tests 2/2; skill-tool tests 13/13. A real MCP smoke discovered `hari-invest` for “估值”, loaded it successfully and found all six logic IDs. After rebuilding, the local runtime returned `hari-invest enabled=true`, Web was the only running channel, 3000/3001 were healthy and active chats were zero.

## D-2026-08-10-02 Make Local Dev Login Explicit, Server-owned And Fail-closed

- Status: Accepted and implemented locally; not deployed to production.
- Decision: local source environments may opt in to a test-account login with `HONE_PUBLIC_DEV_LOGIN=true`. Availability requires all three conditions: the flag is truthy, deployment mode is `local`, and effective cloud mode is `local`. Any missing or non-local condition disables the config response and makes the mutation route return `404`.
- Session boundary: the frontend never writes or fabricates cookies. The backend creates or reuses one non-production local identity, records the current TOS version, creates a session through the existing WebAuth storage API, and emits the same HttpOnly/SameSite cookie used by normal login.
- UI boundary: the login form queries the server-owned config and renders the local button only after `enabled=true`. SMS and email login remain unchanged and remain the only public/production login paths.
- Operational boundary: this environment variable is a local developer convenience, not canonical configuration and must never be placed in production service environments, cloud containers or deployment manifests.
- Verification: the pure backend gate test covers missing/false flags, remote deployment and cloud mode; the frontend contract forbids `document.cookie` and requires server config. A real Vite-proxied smoke obtained a normal Cookie and successfully read `/auth/me`; TypeScript, formatting and focused tests passed.

## D-2026-08-11-01 Fail Closed On Unsupported Daily Signals And Stale Valuation

- Status: Accepted and implemented locally; not deployed to production.
- Decision: a dashboard factor exists only when HONE can refresh it from a stable, attributable series. Macro financial conditions therefore use FRED 10Y/30Y Treasury yields, effective Fed funds and VIX with risk-direction scoring, while employment adds the employment-population ratio. AI revenue, RPO/orders, specialized monetization and hardware realization are removed from the model, coverage denominator and UI until reliable first-party evidence exists; an empty placeholder is not a neutral signal.
- Valuation boundary: transcript-era valuation labels and generic P/E buckets are not current valuation. Company-rating valuation is nullable and contributes no score unless the current Beijing day has a `hari-invest-v1`, `verified` bear/base/bull artifact with ordered positive scenarios, non-empty method and assumptions, at least two sources, a generation age under 36 hours and a current price consistent with the market quote. Without it, the 15% valuation weight is removed and all present dimensions are normalized.
- Compatibility boundary: v1 macro, AI and company snapshots are normalized on read. Newly required macro dimensions become unknown, unsupported AI metrics/hardware are removed, and old numeric company valuation is cleared before a stale snapshot can be presented or preserved.
- Presentation: a valid valuation shows bear/base/bull, current price, interval position, method, valuation timestamp and data date. An invalid or absent valuation states “今日不计估值分” and never exposes the old score.
- Verification: Web API 229 passed / 2 ignored; targeted Web contracts 21/21; full Web suite 420/420; TypeScript and production build passed. A fresh local worker produced macro/AI model v2 snapshots, and authenticated browser acceptance confirmed all new macro dimensions, no AI hardware placeholder, and the explicit no-valuation state.

## D-2026-08-11-02 Keep Portfolio Context Inside HONE When Analyzing News

- Status: Accepted and implemented locally; not deployed to production.
- Decision: the daily portfolio-news worker may use the configured model to classify news, but it sends only bounded news facts. Actor identity, cost basis, shares, weights and other portfolio rows remain inside HONE. The backend applies position weight only after model analysis to rank the actor's own report.
- Source boundary: a candidate must match a real held ticker, fall inside the 48-hour window and pass the existing source-class/importance checks. Watchlist-only entries are excluded; option positions map to their underlying, and stock plus option weights for that underlying are combined locally. Low-confidence blogs, legal-ad templates and transcript noise cannot become a重点新闻 item.
- Output boundary: model output is accepted only when every event ID exists and every impact, horizon, thesis-effect, attention and confidence enum is valid. If the source is unavailable, no news passes, or the model is unavailable/invalid, the snapshot records that exact state and does not invent an impact or buying/selling instruction.
- Lifecycle boundary: the backend refreshes at 20:00 Asia/Shanghai and atomically stores latest plus dated history under an actor-derived storage key. The authenticated GET endpoint is read-only. If the portfolio changed after a snapshot, old items are withheld until refresh so one actor never sees analysis for holdings they no longer own.
- Product boundary: this is a research reminder, not position management. The UI exposes source links, time, impact, horizon, thesis effect, confidence and “立即复核 / 持续观察 / 无需动作”; sending the saved report to chat explicitly forbids filling missing analysis or automatically changing a position.
- Verification: portfolio-news Rust tests 6/6; Web API 235 passed / 2 ignored; Web dashboard/style contracts included in 425/425; TypeScript and production build passed; authenticated local browser acceptance covered the truthful no-portfolio state.

## D-2026-08-11-03 Make Position Advice Evidence-gated, Deterministic And Non-executing

- Status: Accepted and implemented locally; not deployed to production.
- Decision: the daily position-management report is a deterministic HONE policy over already-cached evidence, not a free-form model answer. It applies Hari `LOG-V0003/4/5/6` to market state, dynamic barbell structure, company/sector budget and thesis falsification, while keeping exact concentration thresholds explicitly owned by HONE rather than attributing them to Hari.
- Current-evidence gate: a transcript research card or old score cannot become a current position action. A company needs a current quote and quarterly financial date to receive a rating-backed action. “加仓候选” additionally requires green quality with fundamentals/scarcity at least 70, a verified same-day Hari valuation at or below base, a current macro green light, position weight below 15%, and no analyzed thesis-weakening news. Any missing requirement collapses to hold or insufficient-data; missing data never becomes zero.
- Risk-action gate: analyzed negative news that weakens the thesis produces immediate review before any other action. Red quality or a price above the verified bull case can produce lower-exposure only when the position is at least 15%. A 25% single position or 45% theme exposure is a HONE high-concentration alert; even a green company is reviewed rather than silently accepted. These labels are research priorities, not orders or promises.
- Privacy and mutation boundary: snapshots are actor-keyed, watchlist rows are excluded, and options aggregate to their underlying. The authenticated API is read-only. HONE does not place orders, change weights or claim execution. A portfolio update invalidates and hides the previous item list until the next refresh.
- Lifecycle: the position report runs after the daily portfolio-news refresh and consumes the latest company-rating, macro and news snapshots. It stores atomic latest plus dated history. If upstream evidence is stale, transcript-only, unavailable or uncovered, that status remains visible in the item and aggregate report.
- Verification: position-policy Rust tests 7/7; Web API 242 passed / 2 ignored; Web 430/430; focused dashboard/style contracts 23/23; TypeScript and production build passed. Authenticated local mobile acceptance covered a real actor portfolio, a high-concentration warning, a non-green macro state, explicit insufficient-data rows, expanded rationale/risks/falsifiers and the non-execution disclaimer without retaining the actor's symbols or weights in repository documentation.

## D-2026-08-11-04 Treat Influencer Content As Attributed Opinion, Not Market Truth

- Status: Accepted and implemented locally; not deployed to production.
- Source boundary: the daily brief accepts only exact registered author/source aliases. SemiAnalysis uses its official RSS feed. By explicit user instruction, Serenity may use the public feed declared by `aichainmap.com/serenity/` as a translation/aggregation layer; the adapter pins the exact HTTPS host/path, applies timeout/size limits and accepts only exact `x.com/aleabitoreddit/status/{id}` original URLs. The UI names both layers. Jukan remains “源待配置”; HONE does not substitute other mirrors, reposts, search snippets or similarly named accounts.
- Analysis boundary: the configured model sees only bounded fetched titles and excerpts and returns a strict ID/enum JSON envelope. Unknown IDs and action-language enums are rejected; security tickers are displayed only when the source text explicitly names them. If the model is absent or invalid, HONE shows source-only material rather than inferred stance.
- Product boundary: every row retains author, public handle, Beijing publish time, original link, fact/opinion label, horizon, HONE summary and a counterpoint. Author opinion is not a HONE conclusion and the saved-report Q&A envelope forbids turning it directly into a buy, sell or position action.
- Lifecycle: refresh once at 19:50 Asia/Shanghai over the prior 36 hours, deduplicate by source URL and store atomic global latest/history snapshots. A snapshot older than 36 hours is labeled stale. The authenticated endpoint is read-only.
- Verification: influencer Rust tests 7/7 within Web API 254 passed / 2 ignored; core config 153/153; Web 436/436; TypeScript and production build passed. A fresh local Web-only run connected Serenity plus SemiAnalysis, preserved nine current X originals, left Jukan unconfigured and kept all rows source-only while no digest model was configured.

## D-2026-08-11-05 Build Key-event Chains From Admitted Sources, Not Narrative Memory

- Status: Accepted and implemented locally; not deployed to production.
- Topic boundary: v1 supports only explicit Rubin/鲁宾 and HBM/high-bandwidth-memory keyword families. A model cannot admit an event into a topic; it may only analyze deterministic matches. This prevents a plausible narrative from becoming a timeline without a source mention.
- Provenance boundary: each event retains source, Beijing time, original URL, title and bounded excerpt. Cashtags are extracted only from source text. Translation/aggregation content remains attributed opinion until a primary company, filing or product source confirms it.
- Analysis boundary: optional model output must preserve an admitted event ID and use closed change/direction enums plus bounded impact and next-watch text. Invalid or missing analysis leaves the event visible as source-only with impact explicitly pending primary-evidence verification. Source failure is not reported as “no event”.
- Lifecycle and product boundary: the global read-only worker refreshes at 19:55 Asia/Shanghai over 30 days and atomically stores latest/history. Snapshots older than 36 hours are stale. The seventh homepage Button can send a saved topic to chat, but cannot convert the chain directly into a trade or position action.
- Verification: key-chain Rust tests 5/5 within Web API 254 passed / 2 ignored; Core 153/153; Web 436/436; TypeScript and production build passed. A fresh local run produced four Rubin and three HBM events with original X links; because the local digest model is unconfigured, all impacts truthfully remain source-only and pending verification.

## D-2026-08-11-06 Treat The Next Ten Days As A Verification Queue, Not A Prediction Calendar

- Status: Accepted and implemented locally; not deployed to production.
- Product boundary: the continuous-research follow-up lives inside the existing key-event-chain detail as a “十日简报” view. It does not create another homepage launcher. The prior ten calendar days are an evidence review; the following ten days are an open-question queue.
- Time boundary: past review includes only events whose Beijing publish date falls inside the ten-day window and keeps their existing evidence IDs and original links. `review_by` is the date HONE should revisit a question, not a claim that an event will happen on that date.
- Evidence boundary: a future question can be `open` only when the current ten-day window has attributable evidence for its topic. Otherwise it is `waiting_for_source`, carries no evidence ID and explicitly says that HONE cannot judge early. Questions never become company events, impact conclusions or trade actions by themselves.
- Version boundary: each refresh compares admitted event IDs with the previous snapshot and reports only genuinely new attributable events. On the first run it creates a baseline rather than calling every historical event new. If a broad source title hides the matched topic, the review headline uses the bounded excerpt sentence containing the admitted keyword instead of inventing a summary.
- Failure boundary: source-unconfigured, source-failed, no-update, source-only and stale states survive into the ten-day view. A source failure is never restated as “nothing changed”, and a missing model leaves direction pending primary-evidence verification.
- Verification: key-event-chain Rust tests 9/9; full Web API 258 passed / 2 ignored; Web 438/438; TypeScript and public production build passed. A fresh local Web-only run produced a source-only brief for 2026-08-02–08-11 and a review queue for 2026-08-12–08-21. Authenticated desktop browser acceptance verified the view switch, one Rubin evidence link, four review questions, two HBM `waiting_for_source` states and the no-prediction copy.

## D-2026-08-11-07 Keep Imported Research Actor-scoped And Evidence-only

- Status: Accepted and implemented locally; not deployed to production.
- Source boundary: HONE accepts user uploads, official exports and future explicitly authorized connectors. Knowledge Planet and IMA are not scraped through a user's private browser session, simulated login or undocumented private endpoint. Every item keeps source name, optional source URL, source date, import type, filename, size and SHA-256; duplicate bytes are not re-imported.
- Scope boundary: personal items are actor-scoped. Only a server-authoritative Web administrator may create, update or delete `hone_global` items. Chat retrieves personal plus global material for the authenticated actor; the shared key-event chain reads global items only; portfolio news reads that actor's personal items plus global items. One user's uploads cannot become another user's evidence.
- Authorization and retrieval boundary: uploading does not authorize every use. Each item explicitly opts into chat, key-event-chain and/or portfolio-news. Chat uses bounded ticker/topic/query relevance. Key-event chains still require deterministic Rubin/HBM admission. Portfolio news requires an exact held-ticker tag and a source date inside its 48-hour window. Missing authorization, metadata or relevance means the item is not consumed.
- Evidence boundary: imported prose is untrusted external evidence, never a system instruction, verified fact or trade action. Server-injected research context tells the Agent to cross-check time-sensitive claims against current first-party sources and cite the material's source/date; the envelope is stripped from user-visible history. Contradictory or stale uploads remain attributable clues rather than silent truth.
- Storage boundary: local mode currently uses atomic manifest files plus uploaded bytes under `storage.sessions_dir/research-library/{personal/<actor>|hone-global}`. This is suitable for the present single-node local HONE. Before any independent multi-instance production rollout, metadata must move to PostgreSQL and bytes to the existing object-store boundary; the current filesystem layout is not a cloud authority and no vector database is claimed.
- Verification: Web API 262 passed / 2 credentialed live tests ignored; Web 441/441; TypeScript typecheck, Rust format check and public production build passed. Authenticated desktop and 390px mobile browser acceptance covered upload, provenance, downstream-use labels, filtering, download/delete controls and zero horizontal overflow. A real API smoke uploaded one IMA-labelled Markdown sample and read it back with correct actor scope and metadata.

## D-2026-08-11-08 Keep Numeric Valuation HONE-owned, Reproducible And Fail-closed

- Status: Superseded locally by D-2026-08-11-10; retained as the historical `hone-valuation-v1` contract.
- Method boundary: daily numeric values are generated by `hone-valuation-v1`, a transparent HONE analytical model. Its five-year fading-growth DCF, 12%/10%/9% scenario discount rates, 2%/3%/3.5% terminal growth rates, growth clamps, forward-EPS multiple formula and 70% DCF / 30% multiple blend are HONE-owned defaults. Hari's framework informs the need to test scarcity, differentiation, reality and remaining upside, but these exact numeric thresholds are not represented as old Wang's reviewed valuation system.
- Input boundary: the worker uses current FMP quote, eight quarterly cash-flow periods, the latest quarterly balance sheet and annual analyst estimates. A quote older than four days, financial input older than 200 days, non-positive current or prior TTM free cash flow, missing shares/net-cash inputs, missing EPS cross-check, cross-check gap above 50%, unordered scenarios or failed reverse-DCF solve blocks eligibility. Missing data is not replaced with a static multiple, transcript-era price or demo value.
- Lifecycle and integration: the global worker runs at 19:20 Asia/Shanghai and atomically stores the full 52-company coverage snapshot. Only `eligible` rows are projected into the existing company-rating valuation file as `hone-valuation-v1 / computed`; the 19:30 company-rating refresh then applies its independent freshness and quote-consistency checks. `verified / hari-invest-v1` remains a separate human-reviewed contract and is not overwritten semantically.
- Product boundary: `/valuation-lab` shows bear/base/bull, current position, reverse-DCF implied growth, cross-check gap, inputs, assumptions, sources and dates. Unsupported business models or unavailable providers remain visible as no-valuation rows. The report is research support, not a guaranteed fair value, trade order or execution path.
- Verification: valuation Rust tests 5/5; full Web API 268 passed / 2 ignored; Web 444/444; TypeScript typecheck, Rust formatting and public production build passed. Authenticated API smoke with no FMP configuration returned 52 explicit unavailable rows and zero fabricated values. Desktop browser acceptance covered the dashboard, filters and expanded failure reason.

## D-2026-08-11-09 Make Decisiveness An Evidence-backed Decision-zone Contract

- Status: Accepted and implemented locally; not publicly released or deployed to production.
- Decision: HONE investment answers express decisiveness through one explicit current research zone — `机会区`, `持有区`, `风险区`, or `数据不足` — plus confidence and one core reason in the first paragraph after the required data-time boundary. They then separate short-, medium- and long-term judgment, cite current evidence, state the strongest contrary case and name observable conditions that would change the conclusion. “果断” means a falsifiable probability judgment, not impersonating old Wang, inventing precision, promising returns or executing a trade.
- Skill boundary: the supplied internal `laowang-investment-internal` v0.4.0 package was reviewed together with the installed v0.4.1 update, but is not copied into the public runtime. `skills/hari-invest` v0.2 contains only the public framework, decision rubric and conversation contract; internal team workflow, private source paths, identity claims and maintenance operations remain excluded. It extends D-2026-08-10-01 rather than replacing its mandatory-load and current-evidence rules.
- Runtime boundary: bounded multi-character Chinese trigger phrases may match inside natural questions so “英伟达现在能买吗” and “回调后可以加仓吗” discover Hari Invest without requiring a slash command. The system fallback independently enforces conclusion-first behavior when investment intent is detected, but neither layer may fabricate absent evidence; critical missing data selects `数据不足`, while secondary gaps lower confidence rather than forcing an empty non-answer.
- Availability and security: Skill installation is only one of three required layers: a safe runnable model channel, the loaded decision Skill, and current evidence tools. The local administrator channel can use the signed-in Codex ACP runner. Ordinary public actors deliberately cannot use that host-capable process and currently fail closed when no actor-safe function-calling provider is configured. Production readiness therefore requires a server-side function-calling model or `hone_cloud`, credentials, readiness monitoring and golden-dialogue evaluation; it must not be achieved by weakening the actor sandbox.
- Verification: Skill Creator validation passed; the static Hari conversation contract passed; natural Chinese discovery tests passed; `hone-tools` completed 185 passed / 1 ignored and `hone-channels` completed 789 passed / 1 ignored; the console binary built. A real local administrator dialogue loaded `hari-invest` plus its decision, voice and boundary references, then answered the no-live-data AI-chip scenario as high-confidence `风险区`, with short/mid/long actions, counterargument and change conditions. A public-dev actor correctly failed before Skill execution because no safe provider was configured, confirming the intended security boundary.

## D-2026-08-11-10 Route Daily Valuation By Business Model And Feed Ratings Only From Cross-checked Results

- Status: Accepted and implemented locally; not deployed to production.
- Method selection: `hone-valuation-v2` replaces the universal 70% DCF / 30% P/E blend. Cyclical manufacturing uses forward P/E, EV/EBIT and cycle-adjusted DCF at HONE defaults of 60%/25%/15%. Profitable growth uses forward P/E, EV/EBIT and DCF at 40%/20%/40%. Revenue-transition names use EV/S as the primary method at 60%, with P/E and/or DCF as independent checks. These are transparent HONE routing defaults inspired by the supplied SNDK analysis; the report's SNDK-specific margins, prices and multiples do not become universal old-Wang parameters.
- Cycle and scenario discipline: cyclical DCF reverts current FCFF toward the median positive annual FCFF across as many as five annual windows, and only cyclicals subtract contract liabilities from net cash. Bear/base/bull scenarios carry 20%/55%/25% probabilities. The lab publishes the probability-weighted value and expected upside, while reverse valuation exposes the current price's implied forward EPS/P/E or solvable cash-flow growth.
- Input and quality boundary: the worker fetches 20 quarterly cash-flow and income-statement periods, a current balance sheet and annual analyst estimates. It skips a fiscal year ending in under 180 days when selecting the next full-year estimate. A computed row requires at least two methods in every scenario, ordered positive scenarios, quote age at most four days, financial age at most 200 days and base-method dispersion at most 50%; otherwise it remains explicit `review_required` or `data_unavailable` and cannot influence a rating.
- Rating integration: the 19:20 Asia/Shanghai valuation worker atomically writes the full lab snapshot and only its eligible `hone-valuation-v2 / computed` rows, then immediately refreshes company ratings. The independent 19:30 rating worker remains. `hone-company-rating-v3` rejects legacy v1 computed rows and scores the 15% valuation dimension from probability-weighted upside, confidence shrinkage and method coverage; a missing or rejected valuation removes that weight rather than manufacturing a neutral score. Human-reviewed `hari-invest-v1 / verified` remains a separate admissible contract.
- Verification: Web API completed 276 passed / 2 ignored, including cyclical and profitable-growth valuation regressions; 22 focused Web tests, TypeScript typecheck, Rust formatting/diff checks, public production build and console binary build passed. A fresh local backend produced `hone-valuation-v2` and `hone-company-rating-v3` snapshots; with no FMP key pool it returned 52 explicit unavailable valuation rows, zero rating valuation rows and no legacy fallback.

## D-2026-08-11-11 Compose Covered-company Dialogue From Historical Thesis Cards And Current Evidence

- Status: Accepted and implemented locally; not deployed to production.
- Corpus boundary: the existing 51 authorized transcripts and four research workbooks remain distilled into 52 private company cards rather than copied wholesale into every prompt. A separate alias index covers ticker, English name and common Chinese name. The current turn projects only explicitly named covered companies, capped at eight, so one company's research cannot silently contaminate another and the full private corpus is not exposed to the model or user on every turn.
- Skill composition: when a card is projected, the runtime must actually load both `company-thesis-ratings` and `hari-invest`. The company Skill has priority over generic model memory for the historical business model, fundamental structure, moat, value-chain position, risks and falsifiers. Hari remains the decision and output layer. Loading only Hari or merely claiming that the company research was used is insufficient for a covered-company answer.
- Truth-source boundary: company cards are dated historical research baselines, not current facts, current ratings or old-Wang latest opinions. Current price, filings, results, guidance, orders, news, industry state and numeric valuation inputs retain the existing current-turn evidence chain. If current first-party evidence conflicts with a card, current evidence wins and the answer states whether the old thesis strengthened, weakened or failed. Historical numbers may not be copied forward as current data.
- Privacy and matching: user-visible answers may summarize the cards but may not reveal transcript text, internal filenames or Skill paths. Chinese aliases and long tickers match deterministically; short tickers such as APP, BE, MU and FN require explicit uppercase syntax unless an unambiguous company-name alias appears, preventing ordinary words from activating private research context. Uncovered companies receive no historical card and use the normal research framework.
- Verification: Skill Creator validation passed for both Skills; the alias/card parity and projection tests passed, including Chinese alias, multiple short tickers, false-positive suppression and an uncovered NVDA case. `hone-channels` completed 793 passed / 1 ignored. Both CI-safe dialogue contracts, Rust formatting/diff checks and the console binary build passed.

## D-2026-08-11-12 Promote External Research Through An Explicit Trust Ladder

- Status: Accepted and implemented locally; not deployed to production.
- Scope boundary: every signed-in user owns a private `personal` knowledge-source domain. A user may copy one successfully parsed personal item into `community_candidate`, but cannot write that domain directly or write `hone_global`. Candidates are visible only to their submitter and server-authoritative administrators.
- Retrieval boundary: `community_candidate` is excluded from the retrieval function used by chat, key-event chains, portfolio news and daily products. An administrator must explicitly approve a candidate; approval copies immutable bytes and provenance into `hone_global`, while rejection retains an auditable state and note. The forum/community content domain remains separate and untrusted.
- Connector boundary: the official Knowledge Planet CLI/Skill uses OAuth on the user's device and stores its Token in that device's system Keychain. HONE therefore accepts a read-only export/sync package produced by that official path, but does not claim server-side multi-tenant automatic sync, accept browser cookies or share one server CLI login across users. iMA remains explicit file export/import until a stable official third-party authorization contract exists.
- Product boundary: `/research-library` is presented as “我的知识源” and is reachable from `/me`; the chat shortcut remains. The UI names the `personal → candidate → official` transition, exposes review state and prevents a failed parse from being submitted.
- Storage boundary: this extends the existing local single-node manifest/file implementation. A production multi-instance release still requires PostgreSQL metadata and object storage for personal, candidate and global bytes before rollout.
- Verification: focused research-library Rust tests 3/3; Web 445/445; typecheck and public production build passed; CI-safe curation contract passed. Authenticated desktop and 390×844 browser acceptance verified connector disclosure, candidate controls, `/me` entry and zero horizontal overflow. The local backend was rebuilt and restarted with dev login enabled.

## D-2026-08-11-13 Model Key Events As A First-principles Industry State Chain

- Status: Accepted and implemented locally; not deployed to production.
- Mainline boundary: the key-event product owns twelve explicit state chains from demand to infrastructure: frontier models, applications, data centers, ASIC/custom silicon, Rubin systems, HBM, HBF, NAND/SSD, 800G/1.6T optical links, CPO, NPO and SOFC. Each topic states its first-principles transmission logic and one bounded validation question. A generic AI mention is not enough to enter a chain.
- Milestone boundary: admission requires both a topic match and one closed milestone class: schedule, specification, launch, qualification, mass production, order, capacity, deployment, financial or policy. Viewpoints and unclear mentions do not become timeline events. Models and applications have additional product/adoption context gates, while 800G/1.6T requires optical context. Missing coverage stays empty rather than being backfilled with narrative memory.
- Truth boundary: confirmation is topic-specific. Only a relevant company-controlled HTTPS host or SEC filing can be `confirmed`; research-library material, Serenity/白毛, SemiAnalysis, X posts and other secondary sources remain `clue` even when useful. Exact official-feed handles and allowed hosts are pinned. The optional model may classify an already admitted ID but cannot admit an event, change its source tier or upgrade a clue to fact.
- Lifecycle and product boundary: the global worker refreshes at 19:55 Asia/Shanghai, keeps 30 days, reserves capacity for confirmed events before clues and atomically stores latest/history snapshots. The UI exposes industry layer, first principle, confirmed/clue counts, source tier, verification note and a confirmed-only filter. The ten-day view keeps at most one question per topic and calls a question open only when it has confirmed primary evidence. Saved chat context preserves these evidence labels and cannot become a trade action.
- Current source posture: only the OpenAI official RSS endpoint was enabled by default after a live HTTPS/XML check. The other company registries are ready but remain unconfigured until each feed or API is individually validated. On the 2026-08-11 local refresh, HONE retained 40 source-linked milestones: 3 confirmed and 37 clues; SOFC correctly remained empty. This is incomplete coverage, not evidence that nothing happened.
- Verification: key-event-chain Rust tests 12/12; full Web API 281 passed / 2 ignored; Web 446/446; focused dashboard tests 6/6; TypeScript typecheck, Rust formatting and public production build passed. Authenticated desktop and 390×844 browser acceptance verified all twelve topics, the confirmed-only filter, twelve bounded ten-day questions, truthful waiting states and zero page/dialog overflow.

## D-2026-08-11-14 Keep Member Discussion Outside The Research Authority

- Status: Accepted and implemented locally; not deployed to production.
- Trust boundary: `/community` now has two explicit domains. “官方动态” remains the read-only HONE-published archive. “讨论区” contains untrusted member speech and never enters Agent prompts, company ratings, daily signals, key-event chains, portfolio news or research-library retrieval. A member who wants a post or attachment considered for official research must separately import it through “我的知识源” and use the existing candidate/administrator promotion workflow.
- Identity and authorization: every forum endpoint requires the paid public session. Public author names are deterministic aliases derived from a domain-separated SHA-256 actor key; phone numbers, email addresses, internal user IDs and report identities are never projected. Authors may delete their own posts/comments. Server-authoritative administrators may hide or restore posts and see aggregate report counts.
- Moderation and abuse boundary: title/body/comment length, tag count and hourly post/comment rates are server-owned. Likes are actor-deduplicated. A member cannot report their own post or report the same post twice; three distinct reports move the post to `pending_review` and hide it from ordinary readers until administrator review. This is a safety threshold, not a truth judgment.
- Attachment boundary: a post may carry one file up to 10 MB. Only PDF, UTF-8 Markdown/text, PNG, JPEG and WebP are accepted, with MIME, extension and file magic checked together. HTML, SVG and executable formats are rejected. Downloads require an authenticated actor, verify SHA-256 before delivery, use `private, no-store`, and render inline only for passive images/PDF.
- Storage boundary: local v1 uses an atomic JSON manifest and separate attachment bytes under the configured session-storage parent. This supports the current single-node local HONE only. Production rollout requires PostgreSQL rows, object storage, retention/deletion policy, backups and moderation operations; the current filesystem is not a multi-instance authority.
- Verification: focused forum Rust tests 7/7; full Web API 286 passed / 2 ignored; focused Web contracts 10/10; full Web 451/451; TypeScript, Rust formatting, public production build and CI-safe research-boundary regression passed. Authenticated browser acceptance created a post, anonymous author alias, normalized tags, like and comment, then verified the cleared empty state and a 390×844 layout with no horizontal overflow.

## D-2026-08-11-15 Separate Weekly Review From The Industry Event Chain

- Status: Accepted and implemented locally; not deployed to production.
- Product boundary: the old ten-day verification queue is no longer exposed inside the key-event-chain API or dashboard. A standalone “周度简报” launcher presents the previous and next Beijing natural weeks. The key-event product returns to its single responsibility: evidence-linked first-principles industry timelines.
- Reading boundary: the weekly UI consumes structured JSON, not the finance-calendar PNG. Desktop shows two readable week columns; narrow layouts use one date-grouped agenda. Every event keeps date, category, importance, source, evidence state, analysis and a concrete attention point, so zooming an image is unnecessary.
- Truth boundary: a past calendar date proves only that an important release was scheduled; without the release result it is labeled `schedule_passed` and explicitly says “结果待核验”. A future date is `scheduled`, not a prediction. Only topic-specific company or regulatory evidence already marked `confirmed` may describe an actual industry change. Missing earnings coverage is surfaced and never filled with guessed dates or results.
- Scope and performance: earnings scope is the actor's holdings plus the 52-company HONE research universe. FMP symbol reads are bounded to eight concurrent requests and successful symbol responses are cached for six hours. If the key pool is absent or rejects symbols, macro and confirmed-industry rows remain available while earnings status becomes partial.
- Analysis boundary: category-specific notes explain what policy, inflation, labor, growth and earnings events can transmit into fundamentals or valuation and what primary fields to verify. They do not invent consensus, actual values, guidance or trade actions. Saved-report follow-up requires official macro releases, company IR or filings before answering result-sensitive questions.
- Verification: weekly Rust tests 3/3 within Web API 289 passed / 2 ignored; Web 454/454; TypeScript and production build passed. The authenticated local API produced previous week 2026-08-03–08-09 and next week 2026-08-17–08-23 with four past schedules and three future macro events. With no local FMP key, all 53 covered/held symbols remained explicitly missing rather than fabricated. Browser acceptance verified the launcher order, readable dark desktop dialog, enabled follow-up input and corrected theme-token inheritance.

## D-2026-08-11-16 Make The Weekly Brief A Compact AI-aware Decision Agenda

- Status: Accepted and implemented locally; not deployed to production.
- Home hierarchy: every primary dashboard launcher now shares the same 852px content width, 58px row height, icon scale, border radius and spacing. Weekly Brief no longer uses an oversized special card. Daily Valuation Lab and Research Library are visually subordinate, equal-width utility cards on desktop and stack on narrow screens; dashed provisional styling was removed.
- Reading model: the modal is bounded to 1040×820px and shows one agenda at a time through `上周复盘`, `下周关注` and `未来30天 AI`. It defaults to next week. This replaces the oversized two-column poster while preserving structured, selectable text, source links and date grouping.
- AI calendar boundary: the 30-day view combines provider-returned earnings for a bounded important-AI ticker universe with a source-pinned official calendar. For 2026-08-11 the confirmed official set is Hot Chips 2026 on 2026-08-23–25 and NVIDIA FY27 Q2 results on 2026-08-26. Hot Chips also enters the next-week agenda because its opening date lies in that natural week.
- Truth boundary: official dates carry `official_schedule`; they are still schedules rather than outcomes. Unannounced or approximate dates do not receive a fabricated day. Oracle's official “mid-September” wording therefore remains outside the dated list until an exact company IR date exists. Missing FMP coverage remains visible while official pinned dates can still render.
- Maintenance boundary: the pinned conference/company dates are a small auditable fallback, not a complete perpetual calendar. The normal earnings path remains provider-driven. Adding a new pinned event requires an official URL, exact date/range, event-specific first-principles analysis and a regression; secondary calendars cannot silently create official rows.
- Verification: weekly Rust tests 4/4; full Web API 290 passed / 2 ignored; weekly Web 5/5, TypeScript, production build and Rust formatting/diff checks passed. The rebuilt local Web-only backend returned four next-week items including Hot Chips and two 30-day AI items. Authenticated browser acceptance verified the unified home hierarchy, three tabs, official source links, NVIDIA and Hot Chips content, and no console error. The repository-wide Web command is not counted as green in this change: it currently reports 436 pass plus four existing Markdown-rendering failures/two setup errors because `DOMPurify.sanitize` is undefined under the local Bun test runtime; the weekly test is independently green and this change does not touch the shared Markdown renderer.

## D-2026-08-11-17 Separate Daily Tools From The Conversation Reading Surface

- Status: Superseded locally by D-2026-08-11-18; retained as the short-lived collapsible-panel decision.
- Layout boundary: the nine daily tools live in one named feature panel above the conversation. The panel is open for an empty conversation, collapses when history arrives or a new message is added, and remains manually expandable. When expanded, its list scrolls inside a flex-bounded container; the desktop conversation retains at least 180px and the composer remains a non-overlapping sibling. Mobile keeps the existing fixed composer but reserves 136px below messages.
- Accessibility baseline: the middle chat-font preference now renders assistant prose at 18px/1.75 and user prompts at 17.5px. Tool titles/subtitles use 16px/13px, interactive controls are at least 40–44px high, and weekly/key-event/influencer detail copy was raised from poster-sized microcopy to readable interface sizes. Small, medium, large and extra-large preferences remain available.
- Language boundary: visible navigation and editorial eyebrow copy is Chinese, including “投资助手”, dashboard principles and “北京时间”. HONE, HARI, AI and finance/technology abbreviations such as DCF, VIX, HBM and ticker symbols remain intentionally unchanged. English locale resources and internal schema keys remain available but are not shown in the Chinese interface by default.
- Verification: focused Web contracts 38/38, TypeScript typecheck and production build passed. Authenticated browser acceptance at 1280×720 measured zero message/composer overlap both collapsed and expanded; after the flex containment fix, the expanded tool list also measured zero overflow beyond its panel. The collapsed conversation retained a 474px reading area; expanded tools retained a 198px conversation area and scrolled internally. Weekly dialog browser checks measured a 28px title, 17px event title, 14px body, 13px tabs and no obsolete English eyebrow copy.

## D-2026-08-11-18 Keep Investment Tools Visible As A Horizontal Composer Shelf

- Status: Accepted and implemented locally; not deployed to production.
- Interaction decision: the collapsible dashboard panel from D-2026-08-11-17 is removed. All ten current functions render as independent compact buttons in one horizontal shelf immediately above the composer. The shelf remains visible in both active and empty conversations, scrolls horizontally with touch/trackpad, has no vertical wrapping, and resets toward the first tool when a new conversation starts. Dashboard buttons keep their existing modal and explicit close control; valuation and research-library utilities keep their existing full-page destinations.
- Conversation decision: “新对话” creates a new visible client segment inside the current server-owned session. Historical messages remain recoverable from the sidebar, while the new segment starts blank. Sending a first question appends into that segment without deleting the actor's retained history. A running turn cannot be hidden by starting another visible segment.
- Engagement decision: an empty segment shows exactly five research hooks: macro regime, portfolio priority, next official calendar event, key-event chain and valuation. Portfolio/valuation prompts use only symbols returned by the actor's finance-calendar payload; the calendar prompt uses the nearest returned future event and explicitly forbids guessing unreleased results. Missing holdings or calendar data falls back to generic but still actionable questions.
- Layout boundary: desktop shelf height is 66px with 144×52px cards; all ten cards share one top coordinate. At 1280×720 the shelf measured 948px visible width against 1527px scroll width, showing six complete tools plus part of the seventh. The composer dock measured 197px, the message area retained 467px, and both message/composer overlap and page-level horizontal overflow were zero. Narrow layouts use a 64px shelf with 126×50px cards and reserve 218px below the message scroller for the fixed shelf/composer stack.
- Verification: focused Web and prompt regression tests 26/26, TypeScript typecheck, production build and diff check passed. Authenticated browser acceptance verified ten one-row tool buttons, six fully visible desktop buttons plus a seventh partial button, horizontal scrolling, retained dashboard open/close behaviour, five empty-conversation hooks, zero page-level horizontal overflow and zero composer overlap.

## D-2026-08-11-19 Separate Research Grades From Daily Valuation Evidence

- Status: Accepted and implemented locally; not deployed to production.
- Incident finding: valuation-lab-to-rating integration was already wired and ran in the correct 19:20 → immediate rating-refresh sequence, but the local FMP single key and key pool were both empty. The 2026-08-11 snapshot therefore truthfully contained zero calculated valuations and `data/company_ratings/valuations/latest.json` contained zero eligible rows. Company ratings were showing only transcript research grades; no valuation had been silently copied from old company analyses.
- Scoring decision: the source material's ordinal 1–5 research grades no longer become 20/40/60/80/100 pseudo-percentages. `hone-company-rating-v4` maps them to explicit 25/45/60/75/90 anchors. Grade 5 means highest evidence tier within the imported research, not a perfect company; current financial evidence may adjust fundamentals but is capped at 95. Market confirmation and eligible valuation retain their bounded dynamic scales.
- Formula and missing-data boundary: overall weights remain moat 20%, scarcity 20%, fundamentals 20%, order/execution visibility 15%, valuation 15% and market confirmation 10%. Only same-day, multi-method valuation rows that pass freshness, source and dispersion gates receive the valuation weight. Missing valuation removes and renormalizes that weight rather than using an old target, a neutral 50 or a zero.
- Product explanation: the rating dialog now exposes the five anchors, exact formula and current valuation coverage. Each score is labeled `研究基线分`, `暂未含估值` or `含当日估值`; company detail states whether quote, financial, method-count or review gates prevented valuation. The top status includes quote, financial and valuation coverage instead of hiding the zero-input condition behind a generic baseline label.
- Verification: company-rating Rust tests 12/12, valuation-lab Rust tests 12/12, focused Web regression tests 26/26, TypeScript typecheck, production Web build, console binary build, formatting and diff checks passed. The rebuilt local backend generated `hone-company-rating-v4` at 2026-08-11 21:40 Beijing: valuation coverage remained correctly 0/52 because no provider key exists, while AVGO's four former 100 research dimensions became 90 and its baseline score changed from 95.3 to 86.5. Authenticated browser acceptance verified the visible 0/52 explanation, full scoring rubric, per-row basis label and company-specific missing-input reason.

## D-2026-08-11-20 Score Structure, Financial Delivery And Valuation Separately

- Status: Accepted and implemented locally; not deployed to production.
- Model decision: `hone-company-rating-v5` uses eight named factors: business model/moat 20%, product/market position 10%, growth quality 15%, pricing power/gross-margin trend 10%, profitability/financial health 15%, forward visibility 10%, verified daily valuation 15% and timing confirmation 5%. The first, second and structural visibility factors come from dated company research cards; current financial, estimate, valuation and market factors come only from the current evidence chain.
- Calibration decision: dynamic financial factors combine 60% absolute anchors with 40% percentile rank among the same research theme. If a theme has fewer than five covered companies the comparison universe expands to all covered companies; fewer than three usable observations makes the relative component neutral and explicitly reflects sparse coverage. Missing factors are removed and remaining weights are normalized, never filled with zero, stale research numbers or a manufactured 50.
- Data decision: the valuation worker now derives trailing revenue growth, next-full-year revenue growth, gross margin and year-over-year change, EBIT margin, FCF margin and net-cash-to-revenue from the same statement and estimate calls used by `hone-valuation-v2`. It atomically writes a separate `hone-fundamentals-v1` bridge with dates and provider endpoints. Ratings accept only computed rows generated within 36 hours, no more than 200 financial days old and carrying at least two sources.
- Risk decision: weak moat/product position caps a company at yellow; financial quality below 30 caps it at the red upper bound; valuation below 20 caps it at yellow. The UI names the cap reason, factor coverage, raw financial metrics and as-of date. Forward RPO/backlog/ARR/order evidence uses a separate `hone-forward-evidence-v1 / verified` contract restricted to company-controlled or SEC HTTPS sources; absolute amounts are displayed but only a verified growth rate may adjust visibility. Pipeline or historical transcript numbers are not promoted to current order facts.
- Product decision: the dialog explains all weights, peer calibration, missing-data behavior and caps. Research-only rows are labeled by covered factors rather than presented as complete live ratings. The prior three-scenario valuation method remains unchanged and continues to enter the rating only after its freshness, method-count, source and dispersion gates pass.

## D-2026-08-11-21 Allow A Truthfully Labeled Local Rating Simulation

- Status: Accepted and implemented locally; not deployed to production.
- Purpose: `HONE_COMPANY_RATING_SIMULATION=true` lets the local HONE instance render all eight factors for the 52-company universe so product owners can inspect score dispersion and UI behavior before provider credentials are available.
- Safety boundary: the switch is honored only when deployment and effective cloud mode are both local. The snapshot, list and company detail all state `Codex 模拟预览 / 非真实数据`; the result is not a current rating, quote, financial statement, order fact or investment recommendation.
- Data boundary: deterministic scenario inputs are derived from the dated research-card grades, broad theme priors and fixed ticker jitter. They never enter the real daily evidence chain. Simulated valuation provides only a factor score and explicitly does not fabricate bear/base/bull target prices.
- Exit behavior: removing the environment switch restores the v5 real-data path and its missing-data semantics unchanged. Production must continue to use verified fundamentals, forward evidence, fresh market data and reviewed multi-method valuation.

## D-2026-08-12-01 Normalize Incomplete OpenAI-Compatible Tool Streams At The Adapter Boundary

- Status: Accepted and implemented locally; not deployed to production.
- Incident: GPT Luna accepted HONE's tool schema but omitted `index` on parallel tool-call deltas and omitted a literal finish reason before a clean `[DONE]`. Mapping every missing index to zero concatenated unrelated tool IDs, names and JSON arguments; replaying that malformed assistant tool-call message produced upstream HTTP 400. Separately, blank strings in optional DataFetch refinement-link fields were rejected even though they represented absent optional metadata.
- Decision: the OpenAI-compatible adapter uses each parallel array item's stable position when `index` is absent. The Agent accumulator treats a distinct nonempty tool-call ID on an already occupied index as a new call and keeps later deltas with the same ID on the original call. A clean sentinel after one unambiguous typed finish may synthesize the corresponding internal finish; ambiguous, unfinished or post-finish payloads still fail closed. Blank optional refinement links normalize to absent, while required identity metadata remains nonblank and mutually exclusive links remain invalid.
- Verification: provider parser, stream lifecycle, blank-metadata and reused-index regressions pass. A rebuilt local Web replay of `分析CRWV财报怎么样。` completed successfully in about 36 seconds with separate tool names, one final answer and no HTTP 400.

## D-2026-08-12-02 Keep Daily Investment Dashboards Nonempty Without Manufacturing Evidence

- Status: Accepted and implemented locally; not deployed to production.
- Incident finding: the local FMP key pool was empty. Company ratings therefore had no current quotes, the portfolio-news worker could block the entire snapshot while waiting on its optional LLM, and position management required both a quote and current quarterly financials before emitting any action. The result was 0/52 quote coverage and eight `insufficient_data` positions even though dated research cards and a real portfolio existed.
- Quote boundary: company ratings keep FMP as the preferred provider and use Nasdaq's public official quote endpoint only for missing US-market quotes. The fallback may supply symbol-matched price, change and market date; it may not synthesize financial statements, forward estimates or valuation targets. A failed symbol stays explicitly missing, and valuation remains excluded until the existing same-day multi-method review contract passes.
- News boundary: when FMP news is unavailable, the actor-scoped worker may run bounded Tavily `day` searches for each real holding. Only results whose title explicitly names the ticker or covered company and whose title passes a material-event gate are retained; routine holdings filings, promotional stock articles, generic quote pages and routine RSU/insider records are removed. Tavily content remains a search snippet with its original URL, not a full-page or primary-source fact. Every holding receives one of `news_found`, `no_material_news` or `source_unavailable`.
- Availability boundary: source facts are atomically persisted before optional model analysis. Analysis is time-bounded, and a timeout or provider failure leaves a truthful `source_only` snapshot rather than blocking the whole daily product. Company-rating refresh immediately rebuilds position advice; the position endpoint also rebuilds from newer cached upstream snapshots on demand.
- Action boundary: a symbol with a current quote and dated research baseline may receive a low-confidence `持有 / 立即复核 / 降低暴露` result even when quarterly financials are missing, but it can never become an add candidate without current financial, valuation and macro gates. Unknown symbols remain `数据不足`; HONE never silently rewrites a user's ticker.
- Local acceptance on 2026-08-12: ratings covered 52 companies with 51 current Nasdaq quotes and one explicit unsupported symbol (`SIVEF`); the configured eight-position portfolio received per-symbol news coverage and seven quote-backed position actions. The eighth holding, `APPL`, remained uncovered because it is not a valid covered ticker and was not silently changed to `AAPL`.

## D-2026-08-12-03 Preload Current First-party Evidence Before Investment Reasoning

- Status: Accepted and implemented locally; not deployed to production.
- Evidence decision: company earnings, valuation, cash-flow, capex, backlog and debt questions preload exact-symbol Nasdaq evidence, normalized SEC Company Facts and the latest earnings-like SEC 8-K/6-K/10-Q plus official exhibit before the model reasons. A newer filing date/reporting period outranks lagging Company Facts. Missing fields remain gaps; third-party exact forecasts, targets and scenario values are excluded unless confirmed by full IR/SEC primary evidence.
- Skill decision: interactive investment turns server-preload both `hari-invest` and `company-thesis-ratings`; the current company's dated thesis card is repeated adjacent to the current user turn. The card controls historical business model, moat, valuation method and falsifiers, while current prices and facts retain the first-party chain. Runtime audit, not self-reported prose, proves Skill use.
- Context decision: a weak generic lexical overlap such as “最新” or “分析” cannot inject an unrelated research-library report. Automatic injection requires ticker/topic-strength relevance; explicit “资料库/我上传/我的资料” requests may use broader retrieval. This prevents irrelevant report tickers from expanding a one-company scope and controls Luna prompt size.
- Reliability decision: an OpenAI-compatible streaming request may retry the same client once only when a retryable transport failure occurs before any HTTP response. Once a response/stream starts, it is never replayed. This recovers transient Luna gateway resets without duplicating visible output or business operations.
- Acceptance: the unchanged five-question benchmark scored 432/500, average 86.4, minimum 80, with 5/5 pass, zero hard failures, 100% mandatory-Skill audit coverage and 100% accuracy for reported material facts. GPT-5.6 Sol remains a coverage comparator; primary sources remain the judge.

## D-2026-08-13-01 Promote Causal Links Only Through Repeated Point-in-time Evidence

- Status: Accepted and implemented locally; research/training only, not deployed as execution authority.
- Evidence decision: a structured earnings claim now carries its source event, metric definition/basis, fiscal period and active/corrected/withdrawn disposition. Claims missing the legacy basis remain readable for audit but can never satisfy promotion. Claims are comparable only inside the same metric, definition and period.
- Lifecycle decision: an explicit correction or withdrawal supersedes prior same-key claims. A later SEC filing may supersede a differing earnings release. Other differing primary claims become an unresolved conflict; HONE must not average them, choose the convenient value or let both independently support confidence.
- Promotion decision: one human acceptance is only a label. `hone-causal-promotion-v1` requires two active claims, two distinct source events and HTTPS documents, two fiscal periods, at least 45 days between observations, and explicit acceptance of every active claim. A human rejection or unresolved conflict blocks the driver.
- Policy isolation: promoted causal links may change only the displayed decision confidence. Two promoted drivers raise confidence by at most one level; any conflict or human rejection lowers it by one level. Zone and exposure action are calculated before the adjustment and must remain byte-for-byte equivalent under the same rating, financial and valuation state.
- Time decision: after a causal review is durably appended to the immutable audit and projected into its training sample, HONE creates a new decision revision. The review timestamp becomes part of that new point-in-time state; no later review is rewritten into an older saved decision.
- Review boundary: corrected, superseded, conflicted, withdrawn or legacy-unspecified claims cannot receive an accepted causal-link verdict. The administrator surface shows lifecycle, definition, source-event identity, evidence span and promotion reasons before review.
- Training boundary: this mechanism does not configure a reward, relabel market outcomes, authorize a shadow portfolio, connect a broker or permit autonomous execution.

## D-2026-08-13-02 Backfill SEC Financial Facts as Point-in-time Training Evidence

- Status: Accepted and implemented locally; research/training only.
- Source decision: use the official SEC ticker map, submissions and Company Facts APIs. A fact is admitted only when its accession matches a 10-Q/10-K record and the event links to the official filing document. FMP and LLM prose are not used to create historical reported values.
- Time decision: the SEC acceptance timestamp is the observation time. Filing date is only a fallback when the acceptance timestamp is absent. The current pilot corpus covers SNDK, MU and MSFT to compare one storage company with a memory peer and a non-storage platform company.
